




|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

saṃyuttanikāyai


sagāthāvaggaṭīkā


ganthārambhakathāvaṇṇanā

1. saṃvaṇṇanārambhai  ratanattayavandanā saṃvaṇṇaitabbassa dhammassa pabhavanissayavisuddhipaṭivaidanatthaṃ, taṃ pana dhammasaṃvaṇṇanāsu viñ‍ñūnaṃ bahumānuppādanatthaṃ, taṃ sammadaiva taisaṃ uggahaṇadhāraṇādik‍kamaladdhabbāya sammāpaṭipattiyā sabbahitasukhanipphādanatthanti| atha vā maṅgalabhāvatau, sabbakiriyāsu pubbakic‍cabhāvatau, paṇḍitaihi samācaritabhāvatau, āyatiṃ paraisaṃ diṭṭhānugatiāpaj‍janatau ca saṃvaṇṇanāyaṃ ratanattayapaṇāmakiriyāti| atha vā ratanattayapaṇāmakaraṇaṃ pūjanīyapūjāpuñ‍ñavisaisanibbattanatthaṃ, taṃ attanau yathāladdhasampattinimittakassa kammassa balānuppadānatthaṃ, antarā ca tassa asaṃkaucāpanatthaṃ, tadubhayaṃ anantarāyaina aṭṭhakathāya parisamāpanatthanti idamaiva ca payaujanaṃ ācariyaina idhādhippaitaṃ| tathā hi vakkhati ‘‘iti mai pasan‍namatinau…pai॰… tassānubhāvainā’’ti| vatthuttayapūjā hi niratisayapuñ‍ñakkhaittasaṃbuddhiyā aparimaiyyapabhāvau puñ‍ñātisayauti bahuvidhantarāyaipi laukasan‍nivāsai  antarāyanibandhanasakalasaṃkilaisaviddhaṃsanāya pahauti, bhayādiupaddavañ‍ca nivāraiti| yathāha ‘‘pūjārahai pūjayatau’’tiādi (dha॰ pa॰ 195; apa॰ thaira 1.10.1), tathā ‘‘yai, bhikkhavai, buddhai pasan‍nā, aggai tai pasan‍nā, aggai khau pana pasan‍nānaṃ aggau vipākau hautī’’tiādi (itivu॰ 90)|

‘‘buddhauti  kittayantassa, kāyai bhavati yā pīti|

varamaiva hi sā pīti, kasiṇainapi jambudīpassa|

dhammauti…pai॰…, saṅghauti…pai॰…, jambudīpassā’’ti|| (dī॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.6)|

tathā ‘‘yasmiṃ, mahānāma, samayai ariyasāvakau tathāgataṃ anussarati, naivassa tasmiṃ samayai rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, na dausa…pai॰… na mauhapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hautī’’tiādi (a॰ ni॰ 6.10; 11.11)| ‘‘arañ‍ñai rukkhamūlai vā…pai॰… bhayaṃ vā chambhitattaṃ vā laumahaṃsau na haissatī’’ti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.249) ca|

tattha yassa vatthuttayassa vandanaṃ kattukāmau, tassa guṇātisayayaugasandassanatthaṃ ‘‘karuṇāsītalahadaya’’ntiādinā gāthattayamāha| guṇātisayayaugaina hi vandanārahabhāvau, vandanārahai ca katā vandanā yathādhippaitaṃ payaujanaṃ sādhaitīti| tattha yassā daisanāya saṃvaṇṇanaṃ kattukāmau, sā na vinayadaisanā viya karuṇāppadhānā, nāpi abhidhammadaisanā viya pañ‍ñāppadhānā, atha khau karuṇāpañ‍ñāppadhānāti tadubhayappadhānamaiva tāva sammāsambuddhassa thaumanaṃ kātuṃ tammūlakattā saisaratanānaṃ ‘‘karuṇāsītalahadaya’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| tattha kiratīti karuṇā, paradukkhaṃ vikkhipati apanaitīti atthau| atha vā kiṇātīti karuṇā, paradukkhai sati kāruṇikaṃ hiṃsati vibādhatīti atthau| paradukkhai sati sādhūnaṃ kampanaṃ hadayakhaidaṃ karautīti vā karuṇā| atha vā kamiti sukhaṃ, taṃ rundhatīti karuṇā| aisā hi paradukkhāpanayanakāmatālakkhaṇā attasukhanirapaikkhatāya kāruṇikānaṃ sukhaṃ rundhati vibandhatīti atthau| karuṇāya sītalaṃ karuṇāsītalaṃ, karuṇāsītalaṃ hadayaṃ assāti karuṇāsītalahadayau, taṃ karuṇāsītalahadayaṃ|

tattha kiñ‍cāpi paraisaṃ hitaupasaṃhārasukhādiaparihānicchanasabhāvatāya, byāpādāratīnaṃ ujuvipac‍canīkatāya ca sattasantānagatasantāpavicchaidanākārapavattiyā maittāmuditānampi cittasītalabhāvakāraṇatā upalabbhati, tathāpi paradukkhāpanayanākārappavattiyā parūpatāpāsahanarasā avihiṃsābhūtā  karuṇāva visaisaina bhagavatau cittassa cittapassaddhi viya sītibhāvanimittanti vuttaṃ ‘‘karuṇāsītalahadaya’’nti| karuṇāmukhaina vā maittāmuditānampi hadayasītalabhāvakāraṇatā vuttāti daṭṭhabbā|

atha vā chaasādhāraṇañāṇavisaisanibandhanabhūtā sātisayaṃ niravasaisañ‍ca sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ viya savisayabyāpitāya mahākaruṇābhāvamupagatā karuṇāva bhagavatau abhisayaina hadayasītalabhāvahaitūti āha ‘‘karuṇāsītalahadaya’’nti|

atha vā satipi maittāmuditānaṃ sātisayai hadayasītibhāvanibandhanattai sakalabuddhaguṇavisaisakāraṇatāya tāsampi kāraṇanti karuṇāva bhagavatau ‘‘hadayasītalabhāvakāraṇa’’nti vuttā| karuṇānidānā hi sabbaipi buddhaguṇā, karuṇānubhāvanibbāpiyamānasaṃsāradukkhasantāpassa hi bhagavatau paradukkhāpanayanakāmatāya anaikānipi asaṅkhyaiyyāni kappānaṃ akilantarūpassaiva niravasaisabuddhakaradhammasambharaṇaniyatassa samadhigatadhammādhipataiyyassa ca san‍nihitaisupi sattasaṅghāṭasamupanītahadayūpatāpanimittaisu na īsakampi cittasītibhāvassa añ‍ñathattamahausīti| aitasmiñ‍ca atthavikappai tīsupi avatthāsu bhagavatau karuṇā saṅgahitāti daṭṭhabbā|

pajānātīti pañ‍ñā, yathāsabhāvaṃ pakāraihi paṭivijjhatīti atthau| pañ‍ñāva ñaiyyāvaraṇappahānatau pakāraihi dhammasabhāvajautanaṭṭhaina paj‍jautauti pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautau| savāsanappahānatau visaisaina hataṃ samugghāṭitaṃ vihataṃ| pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautaina vihataṃ pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautavihataṃ| muyhanti taina, sayaṃ vā muyhati, mauhanamattamaiva vā tanti mauhau, avij‍jā| svaiva visayasabhāvapaṭicchādanakaraṇatau andhakārasarikkhatāya tamau viyāti tamau| pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautavihatau mauhatamau aitassāti pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautavihatamauhatamau, taṃ pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautavihatamauhatamaṃ| sabbaisampi hi khīṇāsavānaṃ satipi pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautaina avij‍jandhakārassa vihatabhāvai saddhādhimuttaihi viya diṭṭhippattānaṃ sāvakaihi pac‍caikasambuddhaihi ca savāsanappahānaina sammāsambuddhānaṃ kilaisappahānassa visaisau vij‍jatīti sātisayaina avij‍jāppahānaina bhagavantaṃ thaumaintau āha ‘‘pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautavihatamauhatama’’nti|

atha vā antaraina paraupadaisaṃ attanau santānai ac‍cantaṃ avij‍jandhakāravigamassa nibbattitattā, tathā sabbañ‍ñutāya balaisu ca vasībhāvassa samadhigatattā, parasantatiyañ‍ca dhammadaisanātisayānubhāvaina sammadaiva tassa  pavattitattā bhagavāva visaisatau mauhatamavigamaina thaumaitabbauti  āha ‘‘pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautavihatamauhatama’’nti| imasmiñ‍ca atthavikappai ‘‘pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautau’’ti padaina bhagavatau paṭivaidhapañ‍ñā viya daisanāpañ‍ñāpi sāmañ‍ñaniddaisaina, aikasaisanayaina vā saṅgahitāti daṭṭhabbā|

atha vā bhagavatau ñāṇassa ñaiyyapariyantikattā sakalañaiyyadhammasabhāvāvabaudhanasamatthaina anāvaraṇañāṇasaṅkhātaina pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautaina sabbañaiyyadhammasabhāvacchādakassa mauhandhakārassa vidhamitattā anañ‍ñasādhāraṇau bhagavatau mauhatamavināsauti katvā vuttaṃ ‘‘pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautavihatamauhatama’’nti| aittha ca mauhatamavidhamanantai adhigatattā anāvaraṇañāṇaṃ kāraṇūpacāraina sasantānai mauhatamavidhamananti daṭṭhabbaṃ| abhinīhārasampattiyā savāsanappahānamaiva hi kilaisānaṃ ñaiyyāvaraṇapahānanti, parasantānai pana mauhatamavidhamanassa kāraṇabhāvatau phalūpacāraina anāvaraṇañāṇaṃ ‘‘mauhatamavidhamana’’nti vuc‍catīti|

kiṃ pana kāraṇaṃ avij‍jāsamugghātauyaivaikau pahānasampattivasaina bhagavatau thaumanānimittaṃ gayhati, na pana sātisayaṃ niravasaisakilaisapahānanti? tappahānavacanainaiva tadaikaṭṭhatāya sakalasaṃkilaisagaṇasamugghātassa jautitabhāvatau| na hi sau tādisau kilaisau atthi, yau niravasaisaavij‍jāppahānaina na pahīyatīti|

atha vā vij‍jā viya sakalakusaladhammasamuppattiyā niravasaisākusaladhammanibbattiyā saṃsārappavattiyā ca avij‍jā padhānakāraṇanti tabbighātavacanaina sakalasaṃkilaisagaṇasamugghātau vuttau aiva hautīti vuttaṃ ‘‘pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautavihatamauhatama’’nti|

narā ca amarā ca narāmarā, saha narāmaraihīti sanarāmarau, sanarāmarau ca sau laukau cāti sanarāmaralaukau, tassa garūti sanarāmaralaukagaru, taṃ sanarāmaralaukagaruṃ| aitaina daivamanussānaṃ viya tadavasiṭṭhasattānampi yathārahaṃ guṇavisaisāvahatāya bhagavatau upakārataṃ dassaiti| na caittha padhānāppadhānabhāvau caudaitabbau| añ‍ñau hi saddak‍kamau, añ‍ñau atthak‍kamau| aidisaisu hi samāsapadaisu padhānampi appadhānaṃ viya niddisīyati yathā ‘‘sarājikāya parisāyā’’ti  (apa॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.1.82)| kāmañ‍caittha sattasaṅkhāraukāsavasaina tividhau laukau, garubhāvassa pana adhippaitattā garukaraṇasamatthassaiva sattalaukassa vasaina atthau gahaitabbau| sau hi laukīyanti aittha puñ‍ñapāpāni tabbipākau cāti ‘‘laukau’’ti vuc‍cati| amaraggahaṇaina caittha upapattidaivā adhippaitā|

atha  vā samūhatthau lauka-saddau samudāyavasaina laukīyati pañ‍ñāpīyatīti| saha naraihīti sanarā, sanarā ca tai amarā cāti sanarāmarā, taisaṃ laukauti sanarāmaralaukauti purimanayainaiva yaujaitabbaṃ| amara-saddaina caittha visuddhidaivāpi saṅgayhanti| tai hi maraṇābhāvatau paramatthatau amarā, narāmarānaṃyaiva gahaṇaṃ uk‍kaṭṭhaniddaisavasaina yathā ‘‘satthā daivamanussāna’’nti (dī॰ ni॰ 1.157)| tathā hi sabbānatthapariharaṇapubbaṅgamāya niravasaisahitasukhavidhānatapparāya niratisayāya payaugasampattiyā sadaivamanussāya pajāya ac‍cantamupakāritāya aparimitanirupamappabhāvaguṇavisaisasamaṅgitāya ca sabbasattuttamau bhagavā aparimāṇāsu laukadhātūsu aparimāṇānaṃ sattānaṃ uttamaṃ gāravaṭṭhānaṃ| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘sanarāmaralaukagaru’’nti|

saubhanaṃ gataṃ gamanaṃ aitassāti sugatau| bhagavatau hi vainaiyyajanupasaṅkamanaṃ aikantaina taisaṃ hitasukhanipphādanatau saubhanaṃ, tathā lakkhaṇānubyañ‍janapaṭimaṇḍitarūpakāyatāya dutavilambitakhalitānukaḍḍhananippīḷanuk‍kuṭikakuṭilākulatādi- dausarahitamavahasitarājahaṃsavasabhavāraṇamigarājagamanaṃ kāyagamanaṃ ñāṇagamanañ‍ca vipulanimmalakaruṇāsativīriyādiguṇavisaisasahitamabhinīhāratau yāva mahābaudhi niravaj‍jatāya saubhanamaivāti|

atha vā sayambhūñāṇaina sakalamapi laukaṃ pariñ‍ñābhisamayavasaina parijānantau ñāṇaina sammā gatau avagatauti sugatau, tathā laukasamudayaṃ pahānābhisamayavasaina pajahantau anuppattidhammataṃ āpādaintau sammā gatau atītauti sugatau, laukaniraudhaṃ nibbānaṃ sacchikiriyābhisamayavasaina sammā gatau adhigatauti sugatau, laukaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ bhāvanābhisamayavasaina sammā  gatau paṭipan‍nauti sugatau| ‘‘sautāpattimaggaina yai kilaisā pahīnā, tai kilaisai na punaiti na pac‍caiti na pac‍cāgacchatīti sugatau’’tiādinā nayaina (mahāni॰ 38) ayamatthau vibhāvaitabbauti|

atha vā sundaraṃ ṭhānaṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ, nibbānamaiva vā gatau adhigatauti sugatau| yasmā vā bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ atthasaṃhitaṃ vainaiyyānaṃ yathārahaṃ kālayuttamaiva ca dhammaṃ bhāsati, tasmā sammā gadati vadatīti sugatau da-kārassa ta-kāraṃ katvā| iti saubhanagamanatādīhi sugatau, taṃ sugataṃ|

puñ‍ñapāpakaihi upapaj‍janavasaina gantabbatau gatiyau, upapattibhavavisaisā| tā pana nirayādivasaina pañ‍cavidhā| tā hi sakalassapi bhavagāmikammassa ariyamaggādhigamaina avipākārahabhāvakaraṇaina  nivattitattā bhagavā pañ‍cahipi gatīhi suṭṭhu muttau visaṃyuttauti āha ‘‘gativimutta’’nti| aitaina bhagavatau katthacipi apariyāpan‍nataṃ dassaiti, yatau bhagavā ‘‘daivātidaivau’’ti vuc‍cati| taināha –

‘‘yaina daivūpapatyassa, gandhabbau vā vihaṅgamau|

yakkhattaṃ yaina gacchaiyyaṃ, manussattañ‍ca abbajai|

tai mayhaṃ āsavā khīṇā, viddhastā vinaḷīkatā’’ti|| (a॰ ni॰ 4.36)|

taṃtaṃgatisaṃvattanakānañhi kammakilaisānaṃ aggamaggaina baudhimūlaiyaiva suppahīnattā natthi bhagavatau gatipariyāpan‍natāti ac‍cantamaiva bhagavā sabbabhavayaunigativiñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitisattāvāsasattanikāyaihi suparimuttau| taṃ gativimuttaṃ| vandaiti namāmi, thaumaimīti vā atthau|

atha vā gativimuttanti anupādisaisanibbānadhātuppattiyā bhagavantaṃ thaumaiti| aittha hi dvīhi ākāraihi bhagavatau thaumanā vaiditabbā attahitasampattitau parahitapaṭipattitau ca| taisu attahitasampatti anāvaraṇañāṇādhigamatau, savāsanānaṃ sabbaisaṃ kilaisānaṃ ac‍cantapahānatau, anupādisaisanibbānappattitau ca vaiditabbā| parahitapaṭipatti lābhasak‍kārādinirapaikkhacittassa sabbadukkhaniyyānikadhammadaisanatau, viruddhaisupi nic‍caṃ hitajjhāsayavasaina ñāṇaparipākakālāgamanatau ca| sā panaittha āsayatau payaugatau ca duvidhā parahitapaṭipatti, tividhā  ca attahitasampatti pakāsitā hauti, kathaṃ? ‘‘karuṇāsītalahadaya’’nti aitaina āsayatau parahitapaṭipatti, sammāgadanatthaina sugata-saddaina payaugatau parahitapaṭipatti, ‘‘pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautavihatamauhatamaṃ gativimutta’’nti aitaihi catusac‍capaṭivaidhatthaina ca sugata-saddaina tividhāpi attahitasampatti, avasiṭṭhatthaina taina ‘‘pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautavihatamauhatama’’nti aitaina ca sabbāpi attahitasampatti parahitapaṭipatti pakāsitā hautīti|

atha vā tīhi ākāraihi bhagavatau thaumanā vaiditabbā – haitutau phalatau upakāratau ca| tattha haitu mahākaruṇā, sā paṭhamapadaina dassitā| phalaṃ catubbidhaṃ ñāṇasampadā pahānasampadā ānubhāvasampadā rūpakāyasampadā cāti| tāsu ñāṇapahānasampadā dutiyapadaina sac‍capaṭivaidhatthaina ca sugata-saddaina pakāsitā haunti, ānubhāvasampadā pana tatiyapadaina, rūpakāyasampadā yathāvuttakāyagamanasaubhanatthaina sugata-saddaina lakkhaṇānubyañ‍janapāripūriyā vinā tadabhāvatau| upakārau anantaraṃ abāhiraṃ karitvā tividhayānamukhaina vimuttidhammadaisanā| sau sammāgadanatthaina sugata-saddaina pakāsitau hautīti vaiditabbaṃ|

tattha ‘‘karuṇāsītalahadaya’’nti aitaina sammāsambaudhiyā mūlaṃ dassaiti| mahākaruṇāya sañ‍cauditamānasau hi bhagavā saṃsārapaṅkatau sattānaṃ samuddharaṇatthaṃ katābhinīhārau anupubbaina pāramiyau pūraitvā anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ adhigatauti karuṇā sammāsambaudhiyā mūlaṃ| ‘‘pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautavihatamauhatama’’nti aitaina sammāsambaudhiṃ dassaiti| anāvaraṇañāṇapadaṭṭhānañhi maggañāṇaṃ, maggañāṇapadaṭṭhānañ‍ca anāvaraṇañāṇaṃ sammāsambaudhīti vuc‍catīti| sammāgamanatthaina sugata-saddaina sammāsambaudhiyā paṭipattiṃ dassaiti līnuddhac‍capatiṭṭhānāyūhanakāmasukhal‍likattakilamathānuyauga-sassatucchaidābhinivaisādi-antadvayarahitāya karuṇāpañ‍ñāpariggahitāya majjhimāya paṭipattiyā pakāsanatau sugata-saddassa| itaraihi sammāsambaudhiyā padhānāppadhānabhaidaṃ payaujanaṃ dassaiti| saṃsāramahaughatau sattasantāraṇañhaittha padhānaṃ payaujanaṃ, tadañ‍ñamappadhānaṃ| taisu padhānaina parahitapaṭipattiṃ dassaiti, itaraina attahitasampattiṃ, tadubhayaina attahitāya paṭipan‍nādīsu catūsu puggalaisu bhagavatau  catutthapuggalabhāvaṃ dassaiti| taina ca anuttaradakkhiṇaiyyabhāvaṃ, uttamavandanīyabhāvaṃ, attanau ca vandanakiriyāya khaittaṅgatabhāvaṃ dassaiti|

aittha ca karuṇāgahaṇaina laukiyaisu mahaggatabhāvappattāsādhāraṇaguṇadīpanatau bhagavatau sabbalaukiyaguṇasampatti dassitā hauti, pañ‍ñāgahaṇaina sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇapadaṭṭhānamaggañāṇadīpanatau sabbalaukuttaraguṇasampatti| tadubhayaggahaṇasiddhau hi atthau ‘‘sanarāmaralaukagaru’’ntiādinā vipañ‍cīyatīti| karuṇāgahaṇaina ca upagamanaṃ nirupak‍kilaisaṃ dassaiti, pañ‍ñāgahaṇaina apagamanaṃ| tathā karuṇāgahaṇaina laukasamañ‍ñānurūpaṃ bhagavatau pavattiṃ dassaiti laukavauhāravisayattā karuṇāya, pañ‍ñāgahaṇaina samañ‍ñāya anatidhāvanaṃ| sabhāvānavabaudhaina hi dhammānaṃ samañ‍ñaṃ atidhāvitvā sattādiparāmasanaṃ hautīti| tathā karuṇāgahaṇaina mahākaruṇāsamāpattivihāraṃ dassaiti, pañ‍ñāgahaṇaina tīsu kālaisu appaṭihatañāṇaṃ catusac‍cañāṇaṃ catupaṭisambhidāñāṇaṃ catuvaisāraj‍jañāṇaṃ, karuṇāgahaṇaina mahākaruṇāsamāpattiñāṇassa gahitattā saisādhāraṇañāṇāni cha abhiñ‍ñā aṭṭhasu parisāsu akampanañāṇāni dasa balāni cuddasa buddhañāṇāni sauḷasa ñāṇacariyā aṭṭhārasa buddhadhammā catucattālīsa ñāṇavatthūni sattasattati ñāṇavatthūnīti aivamādīnaṃ anaikaisaṃ pañ‍ñāpabhaidānaṃ vasaina ñāṇacāraṃ dassaiti| tathā karuṇāgahaṇaina caraṇasampattiṃ, pañ‍ñāgahaṇaina vij‍jāsampattiṃ| karuṇāgahaṇaina  attādhipatitā, pañ‍ñāgahaṇaina dhammādhipatitā| karuṇāgahaṇaina laukanāthabhāvau, pañ‍ñāgahaṇaina attanāthabhāvau| tathā karuṇāgahaṇaina pubbakāribhāvau, pañ‍ñāgahaṇaina katañ‍ñutā| karuṇāgahaṇaina aparantapatā, pañ‍ñāgahaṇaina anattantapatā| karuṇāgahaṇaina vā buddhakaradhammasiddhi, pañ‍ñāgahaṇaina buddhabhāvasiddhi| tathā karuṇāgahaṇaina paraisaṃ tāraṇaṃ, pañ‍ñāgahaṇaina sayaṃ tāraṇaṃ| tathā karuṇāgahaṇaina sabbasattaisu anuggahacittatā, pañ‍ñāgahaṇaina sabbadhammaisu virattacittatā dassitā hauti|

sabbaisañ‍ca buddhaguṇānaṃ karuṇā ādi tan‍nidānabhāvatau, pañ‍ñā pariyausānaṃ tatau uttari karaṇīyābhāvatau| iti ādipariyausānadassanaina sabbai buddhaguṇā dassitā haunti| tathā karuṇāgahaṇaina sīlakkhandhapubbaṅgamau  samādhikkhandhau dassitau hauti| karuṇānidānañhi sīlaṃ tatau pāṇātipātādiviratippavattitau, sā ca jhānattayasampayauginīti| pañ‍ñāvacanaina pañ‍ñākkhandhau| sīlañ‍ca sabbabuddhaguṇānaṃ ādi, samādhi majjhai, pañ‍ñā pariyausānanti aivampi ādimajjhapariyausānakalyāṇadassanaina sabbai buddhaguṇā dassitā haunti nayatau dassitattā| aisau aiva hi niravasaisatau buddhaguṇānaṃ dassanupāyau, yadidaṃ nayaggahaṇaṃ, añ‍ñathā kau nāma samatthau bhagavatau guṇai anupadaṃ niravasaisatau dassaituṃ| tainaivāha –

‘‘buddhaupi buddhassa bhaṇaiyya vaṇṇaṃ,

kappampi cai añ‍ñamabhāsamānau|

khīyaitha kappau ciradīghamantarai,

vaṇṇau na khīyaitha tathāgatassā’’ti|| (dī॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.304; 3.141; ma॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 3.425; udā॰ 53; bu॰ vaṃ॰ aṭṭha॰ 4.5; cariyā॰ aṭṭha॰ nidānakathā, pakiṇṇakakathā; apa॰ 2.7.20) –

tainaiva ca āyasmatā sāriputtatthairainapi buddaguṇaparicchaidanaṃ pati anuyuttaina ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’ti paṭikkhipitvā ‘‘api ca mai, bhantai, dhammanvayau viditau’’ti (dī॰ ni॰ 2.146) vuttaṃ|

2. aivaṃ saṅkhaipaina sakalasabbañ‍ñuguṇaihi bhagavantaṃ abhitthavitvā idāni saddhammaṃ thaumaituṃ ‘‘buddhaupī’’tiādimāha| tattha buddhauti kattuniddaisau| buddhabhāvanti kammaniddaisau| bhāvaitvā sacchikatvāti  ca pubbakālakiriyāniddaisau| yanti aniyamatau kammaniddaisau| upagatauti aparakālakiriyāniddaisau| vandaiti kiriyāniddaisau| tanti niyamanaṃ| dhammanti vandanakiriyāya kammaniddaisau| gatamalaṃ anuttaranti ca tabbisaisanaṃ|

tattha buddhasaddassa tāva – ‘‘bujjhitā sac‍cānīti buddhau, baudhaitā pajāyāti buddhau’’tiādinā niddaisanayaina (mahāni॰ 192; cūḷani॰ pārāyanatthutigāthāniddaisa 97) atthau vaiditabbau| atha vā savāsanāya añ‍ñāṇaniddāya ac‍cantavigamatau, buddhiyā vā vikasitabhāvatau buddhavāti buddhau jāgaraṇavikasanatthavasaina| atha vā kassacipi ñaiyyadhammassa anavabuddhassa abhāvaina ñaiyyavisaisassa kammabhāvaina  aggahaṇatau kammavacanicchāya abhāvaina avagamanatthavasainaiva kattuniddaisau labbhatīti buddhavāti buddhau yathā ‘‘dikkhitau na dadātī’’ti| atthatau pana pāramitāparibhāvitau sayambhūñāṇaina saha vāsanāya vihataviddhastaniravasaisakilaisau mahākaruṇā sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇādiaparimaiyyaguṇagaṇādhārau khandhasantānau buddhau| yathāha – ‘‘buddhauti yau sau bhagavā sayambhū anācariyakau pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu sāmaṃ sac‍cāni abhisambujjhi| tattha ca sabbañ‍ñutaṃ pattau balaisu ca vasībhāva’’nti (mahāni॰ 192)| api-saddau sambhāvanai| taina ‘‘aivaṃ guṇavisaisayuttau saupi nāma bhagavā’’ti vakkhamānaguṇai dhammai sambhāvanaṃ dīpaiti| buddhabhāvanti sammāsambaudhiṃ| bhāvaitvāti uppādaitvā vaḍḍhaitvā ca| sacchikatvāti pac‍cakkhaṃ katvā| upagatauti pattau, adhigatauti atthau| aitassa ‘‘buddhabhāva’’nti aitaina sambandhau| gatamalanti vigatamalaṃ, niddausanti atthau| vandaiti paṇamāmi, thaumaimi vā| anuttaranti uttararahitaṃ, laukuttaranti atthau| dhammanti yathānusiṭṭhaṃ paṭipaj‍jamānai apāyatau saṃsāratau ca apatamānai dhāraitīti dhammau|

ayañhaittha saṅkhaipatthau – aivaṃ vividhaguṇagaṇasaman‍nāgatau buddhaupi bhagavā yaṃ ariyamaggasaṅkhātaṃ dhammaṃ bhāvaitvā phalanibbānasaṅkhātaṃ pana dhammaṃ sacchikatvā anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ adhigatau, tamaitaṃ buddhānampi buddhabhāvahaitubhūtaṃ sabbadausamalarahitaṃ attanau uttaritarābhāvaina anuttaraṃ paṭivaidhasaddhammaṃ namāmīti| pariyattisaddhammassapi tappakāsanattā idha saṅgahau daṭṭhabbau| atha vā ‘‘abhidhammanayasamuddaṃ adhigacchi, tīṇi piṭakāni sammasī’’ti ca aṭṭhakathāyaṃ vuttattā pariyattidhammassapi sacchikiriyasammasanapariyāyau labbhatīti saupi idha vuttau aivāti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

tathā ‘‘yaṃ dhammaṃ bhāvaitvā sacchikatvā’’ti ca vuttattā buddhakaradhammabhūtāhi pāramitāhi saha  pubbabhāgai adhisīlasikkhādayaupi idha dhamma-saddaina saṅgahitāti vaiditabbaṃ| tāpi hi malapaṭipakkhatāya gatamalā anañ‍ñasādhāraṇatāya anuttarā cāti| tathā hi sattānaṃ sakalavaṭṭadukkhanissaraṇatthāya katamahābhinīhārau mahākaruṇādhivāsapaisalajjhāsayau pañ‍ñāvisaisaparidhautanimmalānaṃ dānadamasañ‍ñamādīnaṃ uttamadhammānaṃ  satasahassādhikāni kappānaṃ cattāri asaṅkhyaiyyāni sak‍kac‍caṃ nirantaraṃ niravasaisānaṃ bhāvanāpac‍cakkhakaraṇaihi kammādīsu adhigatavasibhāvau acchariyācintaiyyamahānubhāvau adhisīlādhicittānaṃ paramuk‍kaṃsapāramippattau bhagavā pac‍cayākārai catuvīsatikauṭisatasahassamukhaina mahāvajirañāṇaṃ paisaitvā anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhauti|

aittha ca ‘‘bhāvaitvā’’ti aitaina vij‍jāsampadāya dhammaṃ thaumaiti, ‘‘sacchikatvā’’ti aitaina vimuttisampadāya| tathā paṭhamaina jhānasampadāya, dutiyaina vimaukkhasampadāya| paṭhamaina vā samādhisampadāya, dutiyaina samāpattisampadāya| atha vā paṭhamaina khayaiñāṇabhāvaina, dutiyaina anuppādaiñāṇabhāvaina| purimaina vā vij‍jūpamatāya, dutiyaina vajirupamatāya| purimaina vā virāgasampattiyā, dutiyaina niraudhasampattiyā| tathā paṭhamaina niyyānabhāvaina, dutiyaina nissaraṇabhāvaina| paṭhamaina vā haitubhāvaina, dutiyaina asaṅkhatabhāvaina| paṭhamaina vā dassanabhāvaina, dutiyaina vivaikabhāvaina| paṭhamaina vā adhipatibhāvaina, dutiyaina amatabhāvaina dhammaṃ thaumaiti| atha vā ‘‘yaṃ dhammaṃ bhāvaitvā buddhabhāvaṃ upagatau’’ti aitaina svākkhātatāya dhammaṃ thaumaiti, ‘‘sacchikatvā’’ti aitaina sandiṭṭhikatāya| tathā purimaina akālikatāya, pacchimaina aihipassikatāya| purimaina vā aupanaiyyikatāya, pacchimaina pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbatāya dhammaṃ thaumaiti| ‘‘gatamala’’nti iminā saṃkilaisābhāvadīpanaina dhammassa parisuddhataṃ dassaiti| ‘‘anuttara’’nti aitaina añ‍ñassa visiṭṭhassa abhāvadīpanaina vipulaparipuṇṇataṃ| paṭhamaina vā pahānasampadaṃ dhammassa dassaiti, dutiyaina sabhāvasampadaṃ| bhāvaitabbatāya vā dhammassa gatamalabhāvau yaujaitabbau| bhāvanābalaina hi sau dausānaṃ samugghātakau hautīti| sacchikātabbabhāvaina anuttarabhāvau yaujaitabbau| sacchikiriyānibbattitau hi tatuttarikaraṇīyābhāvatau anañ‍ñasādhāraṇatāya anuttarauti| tathā ‘‘bhāvaitvā’’ti aitaina saha pubbabhāgasīlādīhi saikkhā sīlasamādhipañ‍ñākkhandhā dassitā haunti| ‘‘sacchikatvā’’ti aitaina saha asaṅkhatāya dhātuyā asaikkhā sīlasamādhipañ‍ñākkhandhā dassitā hauntīti|

3. aivaṃ saṅkhaipainaiva sabbadhammaguṇaihi saddhammaṃ abhitthavitvā idāni ariyasaṅghaṃ thaumaituṃ ‘‘sugatassā’’tiādimāha| tattha sugatassāti sambandhaniddaisau| tassa ‘‘puttāna’’nti aitaina sambandhau| aurasānanti puttavisaisanaṃ| mārasainamathanānanti aurasaputtabhāvai kāraṇaniddaisau| taina kilaisapahānamaiva bhagavatau aurasaputtabhāvai kāraṇaṃ anujānātīti dassaiti| aṭṭhan‍nanti gaṇanaparicchaidaniddaisau| taina ca satipi taisaṃ sattavisaisabhāvaina anaikasahassasaṅkhābhāvai imaṃ gaṇanaparicchaidaṃ nātivattantīti dassaiti maggaṭṭhaphalaṭṭhabhāvānativattanatau| samūhanti samudāyaniddaisau| ariyasaṅghanti guṇavisiṭṭhasaṅghātabhāvaniddaisau| taina asatipi ariyapuggalānaṃ kāyasāmaggiyaṃ ariyasaṅghabhāvaṃ dassaiti diṭṭhisīlasāmañ‍ñaina saṃhatabhāvatau|

tattha urasi bhavā jātā saṃbaddhā ca aurasā| yathā hi sattānaṃ aurasaputtā attajātatāya pitu santakassa dāyaj‍jassa visaisaina bhāginau haunti, aivamaitaipi ariyapuggalā sammāsambuddhassa dhammassavanantai ariyāya jātiyā jātatāya bhagavatau santakassa vimuttisukhassa ariyadhammaratanassa ca aikantabhāginauti aurasā viya aurasā| atha vā bhagavatau dhammadaisanānubhāvainaiva ariyabhūmiṃ auk‍kamamānā auk‍kantā ca ariyasāvakā bhagavatau uraina vāyāmajanitābhijātitāya nippariyāyaina aurasā puttāti vattabbataṃ arahanti| sāvakaihi pavattiyamānāpi hi dhammadaisanā ‘‘bhagavatau dhammadaisanā’’ic‍caiva vuc‍cati tammūlikattā lakkhaṇādivisaisābhāvatau ca|

yadipi ariyasāvakānaṃ ariyamaggādhigamasamayai bhagavatau viya tadantarāyakaraṇatthaṃ daivaputtamārau, māravāhinī vā na aikantaina apasādaiti, taihi pana apasādaitabbatāya kāraṇai vimathitai taipi vimathitā aiva nāma hauntīti āha ‘‘mārasainamathanāna’’nti| imasmiṃ panatthai ‘‘māramārasainamathanāna’’nti vattabbai mārasainamathanānanti aikadaisasarūpaikasaisau katauti daṭṭhabbaṃ| atha vā khandhābhisaṅkhāramārānaṃ viya daivaputtamārassapi guṇamāraṇai sahāyabhāvūpagamanatau kilaisabalakāyau ‘‘sainā’’ti vuc‍cati| yathāha ‘‘kāmā tai paṭhamā sainā’’tiādi (su॰ ni॰ 438)| sā ca taihi diyaḍḍhasahassabhaidā, anantabhaidā vā kilaisavāhinī satidhammavicayavīriyasamathādiguṇapaharaṇaihi audhisau vimathitā  vihatā viddhastā cāti mārasainamathanā, ariyasāvakā| aitaina taisaṃ bhagavatau anujātaputtataṃ dassaiti|

ārakattā kilaisaihi, anayai na iriyanatau, ayai ca iriyanatau ariyā niruttinayaina| atha vā sadaivakaina laukaina saraṇanti araṇīyatau upagantabbatau, upagatānañ‍ca tadatthasiddhitau ariyā| ariyānaṃ saṅghauti ariyasaṅghau| ariyau ca sau saṅghau cāti vā ariyasaṅghau| bhagavatau  aparabhāgai buddhadhammaratanānampi samadhigamau saṅgharatanādhīnauti assa ariyasaṅghassa bahūpakārataṃ dassaituṃ idhaiva ‘‘sirasā vandai’’ti vuttanti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

aittha ca ‘‘sugatassa aurasānaṃ puttāna’’nti aitaina ariyasaṅghassa pabhavasampadaṃ dassaiti, ‘‘mārasainamathanāna’’nti aitaina pahānasampadaṃ sakalasaṃkilaisapahānadīpanatau, ‘‘aṭṭhan‍nampi samūha’’nti aitaina ñāṇasampadaṃ maggaṭṭhaphalaṭṭhabhāvadīpanatau| ‘‘ariyasaṅgha’’nti aitaina pabhavasampadaṃ dassaiti sabbasaṅghānaṃ aggabhāvadīpanatau| atha vā sugatassa aurasānaṃ puttānanti ariyasaṅghassa visuddhanissayabhāvadīpanaṃ| mārasainamathanānanti sammāujuñāyasāmīcippaṭipan‍nabhāvadīpanaṃ| aṭṭhan‍nampi samūhanti āhunaiyyādibhāvadīpanaṃ| ariyasaṅghanti anuttarapuñ‍ñakhaittabhāvadīpanaṃ| tathā ‘‘sugatassa aurasānaṃ puttāna’’nti aitaina ariyasaṅghassa laukuttarasaraṇagamanasabhāvaṃ dīpaiti| laukuttarasaraṇagamanaina hi tai bhagavatau aurasaputtā jātā| ‘‘mārasainamathanāna’’nti aitaina abhinīhārasampadāya siddhaṃ pubbabhāgai sammāpaṭipattiṃ dassaiti| katābhinīhārā hi sammāpaṭipan‍nā māraṃ māraparisaṃ vā abhivijinanti| ‘‘aṭṭhan‍nampi samūha’’nti aitaina paṭividdhastavipakkhai saikkhāsaikkhadhammai dassaiti puggalādhiṭṭhānaina maggaphaladhammānaṃ pakāsitattā| ‘‘ariyasaṅgha’’nti aggadakkhiṇaiyyabhāvaṃ dassaiti| saraṇagamanañ‍ca sāvakānaṃ sabbaguṇānaṃ ādi, sapubbabhāgapaṭipadā saikkhā sīlakkhandhādayau majjhai, asaikkhā sīlakkhandhādayau pariyausānanti ādimajjhapariyausānakalyāṇā saṅkhaipatau sabbai ariyasaṅghaguṇā pakāsitā haunti|

4. aivaṃ gāthāttayaina saṅkhaipatau sakalaguṇasaṃkittanamukhaina ratanattayassa paṇāmaṃ katvā idāni taṃ nipac‍cakāraṃ yathādhippaitai payaujanai pariṇāmaintau  ‘‘iti mai’’tiādimāha| tattha ratijananaṭṭhaina ratanaṃ, buddhadhammasaṅghā| taisañhi ‘‘itipi sau bhagavā’’tiādinā yathābhūtaguṇai āvaj‍jaintassa amatādhigamahaitubhūtaṃ anappakaṃ pītipāmauj‍jaṃ uppaj‍jati| yathāha –

‘‘yasmiṃ, mahānāma, samayai ariyasāvakau tathāgataṃ anussarati, naivassa tasmiṃ samayai rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, na dausapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, na mauhapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, ujugatamaivassa tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ hauti tathāgataṃ ārabbha| ujugatacittau khau pana, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau labhati atthavaidaṃ, labhati dhammavaidaṃ, labhati dhammūpasaṃhitaṃ pāmauj‍jaṃ, pamuditassa pīti jāyatī’’tiādi (a॰ ni॰ 6.10; 11.11)|

cittīkatādibhāvau  vā ratanaṭṭhau| vuttañhaitaṃ –

‘‘cittīkataṃ mahagghañ‍ca, atulaṃ dul‍labhadassanaṃ|

anaumasattaparibhaugaṃ, ratanaṃ taina vuc‍catī’’ti|| (dī॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 2.33; khu॰ pā॰ aṭṭha॰ 6.3; su॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 226; mahāni॰ aṭṭha॰ 50) –

cittīkatabhāvādayau ca anañ‍ñasādhāraṇā buddhādīsu aiva labbhantīti|

vandanāva vandanāmayaṃ yathā ‘‘dānamayaṃ sīlamaya’’nti| vandanā caittha kāyavācācittaihi tiṇṇaṃ ratanānaṃ guṇanin‍natā, thaumanā vā| puj‍jabhavaphalanibbattanatau puñ‍ñaṃ, attanau santānaṃ punātīti vā| suvihatantarāyauti| suṭṭhu vihatantarāyau| aitaina attanau pasādasampattiyā, ratanattayassa ca khaittabhāvasampattiyā taṃ puñ‍ñaṃ atthappakāsanassa upaghātakaupaddavānaṃ vihananai samatthanti dassaiti| hutvāti pubbakālakiriyā| tassa ‘‘atthaṃ pakāsayissāmī’’ti aitaina sambandhau| tassāti yaṃ ratanattayavandanāmayaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ, tassa| ānubhāvaināti balaina|

5. aivaṃ ratanattayassa nipac‍cakārai payaujanaṃ dassaitvā idāni yassā dhammadaisanāya atthaṃ saṃvaṇṇaitukāmau, tassā tāva guṇābhitthavanavasaina upañ‍ñāpanatthaṃ ‘‘saṃyuttavaggapaṭimaṇḍitassā’’tiādi vuttaṃ, daivatāsaṃyuttādisaṃyuttaihi caiva naḷavaggādivaggaihi ca vibhūsitassāti atthau| tattha  ‘‘saṃyutta’’nti ‘‘saṃyaugau’’ti ca atthatau aikaṃ| kaisaṃ saṃyuttaṃ? suttavaggānaṃ| yathā hi byañ‍janasamudāyau padaṃ, aivaṃ atthaisu ca katāvadhikau padasamudāyau vākyaṃ, vākyasamudāyau suttaṃ, suttasamudāyai vaggauti samañ‍ñā, tathā suttavaggasamudāyai saṃyuttasamañ‍ñā| saṃyuj‍jantīti aittha suttavaggāti saṃyuttaṃ| yadipi avayavavinimuttau samudāyau nāma paramatthatau natthi, avayavai aiva taṃtaṃsan‍nivaisavisiṭṭhai upādāya padādisamañ‍ñā viya suttavaggasamañ‍ñā saṃyuttasamañ‍ñā āgamasamañ‍ñā ca, tathāpi paramatthatau avij‍jamānaupi samudāyau buddhiparikappitarūpaina vij‍jamānau viya gayhamānau avayavānaṃ adhiṭṭhānabhāvaina vauharīyati yathā ‘‘rukkhai sākhā’’ti, tasmā vuttaṃ ‘‘saṃyuttavaggapaṭimaṇḍitassā’’ti|

nanu saṃyuttavaggau aiva āgamau, tassa pana kaihi maṇḍananti? na caudaitabbamaitaṃ| bhavati hi abhin‍naipi vatthusmiṃ yathādhippaitavisaisāvabaudhanatau bhaidakasamudācārau yathā ‘‘silāputtakassa  sarīra’’nti| āgamissanti aittha, aitaina, aitasmā vā attatthaparatthādayauti āgamau, ādikalyāṇādiguṇasampattiyā uttamaṭṭhaina taṃtaṃabhipatthitasamiddhihaitutāya paṇḍitaihi varitabbatau varau, āgamau ca sau varau cāti āgamavarau| āgamasammataihi vā varauti āgamavarau, saṃyuttau ca sau āgamavarau cāti saṃyuttāgamavarau, tassa| buddhānaṃ anubuddhā buddhānubuddhā, buddhānaṃ sac‍capaṭivaidhaṃ anugamma paṭividdhasac‍cā aggasāvakādayau ariyā| taihi atthasaṃvaṇṇanāguṇasaṃvaṇṇanānaṃ vasaina saṃvaṇṇitassa|

atha vā buddhā ca anubuddhā ca buddhānubuddhāti yaujaitabbaṃ| sammāsambuddhainaiva hi vinayasuttaabhidhammānaṃ pakiṇṇakadaisanādivasaina yau paṭhamaṃ atthau vibhattau, sau aiva pacchā taisaṃ atthavaṇṇanāvasaina saṅgītikāraihi saṅgahaṃ āraupitauti| aittha ca saṃyuttānaṃ vaggā samūhāti saṃyuttavaggā, sagāthāvaggādayau| tappariyāpan‍natāya saṃyuttaisu vaggā saṃyuttavaggā, naḷavaggādayau| saṃyuttāva vaggā saṃyuttavaggā| tividhaipi tai aikasaisanayaina gahaitvā vuttaṃ ‘‘saṃyuttavaggapaṭimaṇḍitassā’’ti|

tattha sagāthāvaggai tāva aikādasa saṃyuttāni aṭṭhatiṃsa vaggā| nidānavaggai nava saṃyuttāni aikūnacattālīsa vaggā| khandhavaggai aikādasa saṃyuttāni  aikūnasaṭṭhi vaggā| saḷāyatanavaggai nava saṃyuttāni aṭṭhatiṃsa vaggā| mahāvaggai dvādasa saṃyuttāni aṭṭhacattālīsa vaggā| idamaittha saṃyuttantaravaggānaṃ parimāṇaṃ|

ñāṇappabhaidajananassāti paṭic‍casamuppādakhandhāyatanādikathābahulatāya gambhīrañāṇacariyāvibhāvanatau pañ‍ñāvibhāgasamuppādakassa| idha pana ‘‘pañ‍ñāppabhaidajananassā’’ti svāyamāgamau thaumitau, saṃvaṇṇanāsu cāyaṃ ācariyassa pakati, yadidaṃ taṃtaṃsaṃvaṇṇanānaṃ āditau tassa tassa saṃvaṇṇaitabbassa dhammassa visaisaguṇakittanaina thaumanā| tathā hi sumaṅgalavilāsinīpapañ‍casūdanīmanaurathapūraṇīaṭṭhasālinīādīsu ca yathāk‍kamaṃ ‘‘saddhāvahaguṇassa, paravādamathanassa, dhammakathikapuṅgavānaṃ vicittapaṭibhānajananassa, tassa gambhīrañāṇaihi augāḷhassa abhiṇhasau nānānayavicittassa abhidhammassā’’tiādinā thaumanā katā|

6. atthau kathīyati aitāyāti atthakathā, atthakathāva aṭṭhakathā ttha-kārassa ṭṭha-kāraṃ katvā yathā ‘‘dukkhassa pīḷanaṭṭhau’’ti| āditauti ādimhi paṭhamasaṅgītiyaṃ| chaḷabhiñ‍ñatāya paramaina cittavasibhāvaina saman‍nāgatattā jhānādīsu pañ‍cavidhavasitāsambhāvatau ca vasinau, thairā mahākassapādayau| taisaṃ sataihi pañ‍cahi| yāti yā aṭṭhakathā| saṅgītāti atthaṃ kathaituṃ yuttaṭṭhānai ‘‘ayaṃ aitassa atthau, ayaṃ aitassa atthau’’ti saṅgahaitvā vuttā| anusaṅgītā ca yasatthairādīhi pacchāpi dutiyatatiyasaṅgītīsu| iminā attanau saṃvaṇṇanāya āgamanavisuddhiṃ dassaiti|

7. sīhassa lānatau gahaṇatau sīhaḷau, sīhakumārau, tabbaṃsajātatāya tambapaṇṇidīpai khattiyā, taisaṃ nivāsatāya tambapaṇṇidīpassa ca sīhaḷabhāvau vaiditabbau| ābhatāti jambudīpatau ānītā| athāti pacchā| aparabhāgai hi nikāyantaraladdhīhi asaṅkaratthaṃ sīhaḷabhāsāya aṭṭhakathā ṭhapitāti| taina mūlaṭṭhakathā sabbasādhāraṇā na hautīti idaṃ atthappakāsanaṃ aikantaina karaṇīyanti dassaiti| tainaivāha ‘‘dīpavāsīnamatthāyā’’ti| aittha dīpavāsīnanti jambudīpavāsīnaṃ, sīhaḷadīpavāsīnaṃ vā atthāya sīhaḷabhāsāya ṭhapitāti yaujanā|

8. apanaitvāti  kañ‍cukasadisaṃ sīhaḷabhāsaṃ apanaitvā| tatauti aṭṭhakathātau| ahanti attānaṃ niddisati| manauramaṃ bhāsanti māgadhabhāsaṃ| sā hi sabhāvaniruttibhūtā paṇḍitamanaṃ ramayati| tainaivāha ‘‘tantinayānucchavika’’nti, pāḷigatiyā anulaumikaṃ pāḷichāyānuvidhāyininti atthau| vigatadausanti asabhāvaniruttibhāsantararahitaṃ|

9. samayaṃ avilaumaintauti siddhantaṃ aviraudhaintau| aitaina atthadausābhāvamāha| aviruddhattā aiva hi thairavādāpi idha pakāsīyissanti| thairavaṃsadīpānanti thiraihi sīlakkhandhādīhi saman‍nāgatattā thairā, mahākassapādayau| taihi āgatā ācariyaparamparā thairavaṃsau, tappariyāpan‍nā hutvā āgamādhigamasampan‍nattā pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautaina tassa samuj‍jalanatau thairavaṃsadīpā, mahāvihāravāsinau, taisaṃ| vividhaihi ākāraihi nicchīyatīti vinicchayau, gaṇṭhiṭṭhānaisu khilamaddanākāraina pavattā vimaticchaidanī kathā| suṭṭhunipuṇau saṇhau vinicchayau aitaisanti sunipuṇavinicchayā| atha vā vinicchinautīti vinicchayau vuttappakāravisayaṃ ñāṇaṃ| suṭṭhu nipuṇau chaikau vinicchayau aitaisanti yaujaitabbaṃ| aitaina mahākassapādithairaparamparāgatau, tatau aiva ca aviparītau saṇhau sukhumau mahāvihāravāsīnaṃ vinicchayau, tassa pamāṇabhūtataṃ dassaiti|

10. sujanassa cāti ca-saddau sampiṇḍanatthau| taina ‘‘na kaivalaṃ jambudīpavāsīnamaiva atthāya, atha khau sādhujanatausanatthañ‍cā’’ti dassaiti| taina ca ‘‘tambapaṇṇidīpavāsīnampi atthāyā’’ti ayamatthau siddhau hauti uggahaṇādisukaratāya taisampi bahukārattā| ciraṭṭhitatthanti ciraṭṭhitiatthaṃ, cirakālappavattanāyāti atthau| idañhi atthappakāsanaṃ aviparītapadabyañ‍janasunikkhaipassa atthasunayassa ca upāyabhāvatau saddhammassa ciraṭṭhitiyā pavattati| vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā ‘‘dvaimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā saddhammassa ṭhitiyā asammausāya anantaradhānāya saṃvattanti| katamai dvai? sunikkhittañ‍ca padabyañ‍janaṃ atthau ca sunītau’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 2.20)|

11-12. yaṃ atthavaṇṇanaṃ kattukāmau, tassā mahattaṃ pariharituṃ ‘‘sāvatthipabhūtīna’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| tainaivāha – ‘‘na idha bhiyyau vitthārakathaṃ  karissāmi, na taṃ idha vicārayissāmī’’ti ca| saṅgītīnaṃ dvin‍nanti dīghamajjhimanikāyānaṃ|

13. ‘‘na idha bhiyyau vitthārakathaṃ karissāmī’’ti sāmañ‍ñatau vuttassa atthassa avassayaṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘suttānaṃ panā’’tiādi vuttaṃ|

14. yaṃ aṭṭhakathaṃ kattukāmau, tadaikadaisabhāvaina visuddhimaggau gahaitabbauti kathikānaṃ upadaisaṃ karauntau tatta vicāritadhammai uddaisavasaina dassaiti ‘‘sīlakathā’’tiādinā| tattha sīlakathāti cārittavārittādivasaina sīlassa vitthārakathā| dhutadhammāti piṇḍapātikaṅgādayau tairasa kilaisadhunanakadhammā| kammaṭṭhānāni sabbānīti pāḷiyaṃ āgatāni aṭṭhattiṃsa, aṭṭhakathāyaṃ dvaiti niravasaisāni yaugakammassa bhāvanāya pavattiṭṭhānāni| cariyāvidhānasahitauti rāgacariyādīnaṃ sabhāgādividhānaina sahitau| jhānāni cattāri rūpāvacarajjhānāni, samāpattiyau catassau arūpasamāpattiyau| aṭṭhapi vā paṭiladdhamattāni jhānāni, samāpaj‍janavasibhāvappattiyā samāpattiyau| jhānāni vā rūpārūpāvacarajjhānāni, samāpattiyau phalasamāpattiniraudhasamāpattiyau|

15. laukiyalaukuttarabhaidā cha abhiñ‍ñāyau sabbā abhiñ‍ñāyau| ñāṇavibhaṅgādīsu āgatanayaina aikavidhādinā pañ‍ñāya saṅkalaitvā sampiṇḍaitvā nicchayau pañ‍ñāsaṅkalananicchayau|

16. pac‍cayadhammānaṃ haituādīnaṃ pac‍cayuppan‍nadhammānaṃ haitupac‍cayādibhāvau pac‍cayākārau, tassa daisanā pac‍cayākāradaisanā, paṭic‍casamuppādakathāti atthau| sā pana nikāyantaraladdhisaṅkararahitatāya suṭṭhuparisuddhā, ghanavinibbhaugassa ca suduk‍karatāya nipuṇā saṇhasukhumā, aikattanayādisahitā  ca tattha vicāritāti āha ‘‘suparisuddhanipuṇanayā’’ti| paṭisambhidādīsu āgatanayaṃ avisaj‍jaitvāva vicāritattā avimuttatantimaggā|

17. iti pana sabbanti iti-saddau parisamāpanai, pana-saddau vacanālaṅkārai, aitaṃ sabbanti atthau| idhāti imissā aṭṭhakathāya| na taṃ vicārayissāmi punaruttibhāvatauti adhippāyau|

18. idāni  tassaiva avicāraṇassa aikantakāraṇaṃ niddhāraintau ‘‘majjhai visuddhimaggau’’tiādimāha| tattha ‘‘majjhai ṭhatvā’’ti aitaina majjhaṭṭhabhāvadīpanaina visaisatau catun‍naṃ āgamānaṃ sādhāraṇaṭṭhakathā visuddhimaggau, na sumaṅgalavilāsiniādayau viya asādhāraṇaṭṭhakathāti dassaiti| ‘‘visaisatau’’ti ca idaṃ vinayābhidhammānampi visuddhimaggau yathārahaṃ atthavaṇṇanā hauti aivāti katvā vuttaṃ|

19. ic‍caivāti iti aiva| tampīti visuddhimaggampi| aitāyāti sāratthappakāsiniyā|

aittha ca ‘‘sīhaḷadīpaṃ ābhatā’’tiādinā aṭṭhakathākaraṇassa nimittaṃ dassaiti, ‘‘dīpavāsīnamatthāya sujanassa ca tuṭṭhatthaṃ ciraṭṭhitatthañ‍ca dhammassā’’ti aitaihi payaujanaṃ, ‘‘saṃyuttāgamavarassa atthaṃ pakāsayissāmī’’ti aitaina piṇḍatthaṃ, ‘‘apanaitvāna tatauhaṃ sīhaḷabhāsa’’ntiādinā ‘‘sāvatthipabhūtīna’’ntiādinā ‘‘sīlakathā’’tiādinā ca karaṇappakāraṃ| haiṭṭhimanikāyaisu visuddhimaggai ca vicāritānaṃ atthānaṃ avicāraṇampi hi idha karaṇappakārau aivāti|

ganthārambhakathāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|



1. daivatāsaṃyuttaṃ

1. naḷavaggau

1. aughataraṇasuttavaṇṇanā

vibhāgavantānaṃ  sabhāvavibhāvanaṃ vibhāgadassanavasainaiva hautīti paṭhamaṃ tāva saṃyuttavaggasuttādivasaina saṃyuttāgamassa vibhāgaṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘tattha saṃyuttāgamau nāmā’’tiādimāha| tattha tatthāti yaṃ vuttaṃ – ‘‘saṃyuttāgamavarassa atthaṃ pakāsayissāmī’’ti, tasmiṃ vacanai| tatthāti vā ‘‘aitāya aṭṭhakathāya vijānātha saṃyuttanissitaṃ attha’’nti aittha yaṃ saṃyuttaggahaṇaṃ kataṃ, tattha| pañ‍ca vaggā aitassāti pañ‍cavaggau| avayavaina viggahau, samudāyau samāsatthau|

idāni taṃ āditau paṭṭhāya saṃvaṇṇaitukāmau attanau saṃvaṇṇanāya tassa paṭhamamahāsaṅgītiyaṃ nikkhittānuk‍kamainaiva pavattabhāvaṃ dassaituṃ, ‘‘tassa vaggaisu sagāthāvaggau ādī’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tattha yathāpac‍cayaṃ tattha tattha daisitattā pañ‍ñattattā ca vippakiṇṇānaṃ dhammavinayānaṃ saṅgahaitvā gāyanaṃ kathanaṃ saṅgīti, mahāvisayattā pūjaniyattā ca mahatī saṅgīti mahāsaṅgīti| paṭhamā mahāsaṅgīti paṭhamamahāsaṅgīti, tassā pavattitakālau paṭhamamahāsaṅgītikālau, tasmiṃ paṭhamamahāsaṅgītikālai|

nidadāti daisanaṃ daisakālādivasaina aviditaṃ viditaṃ katvā nidassaitīti nidānaṃ| yau laukai ganthassa upaugghātauti vuc‍cati, svāyamaittha ‘‘aivaṃ mai suta’’nti-ādikau ganthau vaiditabbau, na pana ‘‘sanidānāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammaṃ daisaimī’’tiādīsu (a॰ ni॰ 3.126) viya attajjhāsayādidaisanuppattihaitu| tainaivāha – ‘‘aivaṃ mai sutanti-ādikaṃ āyasmatā ānandaina paṭhamamahāsaṅgītikālai vuttaṃ nidānamādī’’ti| kāmañ‍caittha yassaṃ paṭhamamahāsaṅgītiyaṃ nikkhittānuk‍kamaina saṃvaṇṇanaṃ kattukāmau, sā vitthāratau vattabbā, sumaṅgalavilāsiniyaṃ  pana attanā vitthāritattā tatthaiva gahaitabbāti imissā saṃvaṇṇanāya mahattaṃ pariharantau ‘‘sā panaisā’’tiādimāha|

1. aivaṃ bāhiranidānai vattabbaṃ atidisitvā idāni abbhantaranidānaṃ āditau paṭṭhāya saṃvaṇṇaituṃ ‘‘yaṃ panaita’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| tattha yasmā saṃvaṇṇanaṃ karauntaina saṃvaṇṇaitabbai dhammai padavibhāgaṃ padatthañ‍ca dassaitvā tatau paraṃ piṇḍatthādidassanavasaina saṃvaṇṇanā kātabbā, tasmā padāni tāva dassaintau  ‘‘aivanti nipātapada’’nti-ādimāha| tattha padavibhāgauti padānaṃ visaisau, na padaviggahau| atha vā padāni ca padavibhāgau ca padavibhāgau| padaviggahau ca padavibhāgau ca padavibhāgauti vā aikasaisavasaina padapadaviggahā padavibhāgasaddaina vuttāti vaiditabbaṃ| tattha padaviggahau ‘‘jaitassa vanaṃ jaitavana’’ntiādinā samāsapadaisu daṭṭhabbau|

atthatauti padatthatau| taṃ pana padatthaṃ atthuddhārak‍kamaina paṭhamaṃ aivaṃsaddassa dassaintau ‘‘aivaṃsaddau tāvā’’tiādimāha| avadhāraṇādīti aittha ādi-saddaina idamatthapucchāparimāṇādiatthānaṃ saṅgahau daṭṭhabbau| tathā hi ‘‘aivaṃgatāni puthusippāyatanāni (dī॰ ni॰ 1.163, 165), aivaṃvidhau aivamākārau’’ti ca ādīsu idaṃ-saddassa atthai aivaṃ-saddau| gata-saddau hi pakārapariyāyau, tathā vidhākāra-saddau ca| tathā hi gatavidhaākārasaddai laukiyā pakāratthai vadanti| ‘‘aivaṃ su tai sunhātā suvilittā kappitakaisamassū āmuttamālābharaṇā audātavatthavasanā pañ‍cahi kāmaguṇaihi samappitā samaṅgībhūtā paricārainti, saiyyathāpi tvaṃ aitarahi sācariyakauti| nau hidaṃ, bhau gautamā’’tiādīsu (dī॰ ni॰ 1.286) pucchāyaṃ, ‘‘aivaṃ lahuparivattaṃ (a॰ ni॰ 1.48), aivamāyupariyantau’’ti (pārā॰ 12) ca ādīsu parimāṇai|

nanu ca ‘‘aivaṃ su tai sunhātā suvilittā, aivamāyupariyantau’’ti aittha aivaṃ-saddaina pucchanākāraparimāṇākārānaṃ vuttattā ākāratthau aiva aivaṃ-saddauti? na, visaisasabbhāvatau| ākāramattavācakau hi aivaṃ-saddau, ākāratthauti adhippaitau yathā ‘‘aivaṃ byākhau’’tiādīsu, na pana ākāravisaisavācakau| aivañ‍ca katvā, ‘‘aivaṃ jātaina mac‍cainā’’tiādīni (dha॰ pa॰ 53) upamādīsu udāharaṇāni upapan‍nāni haunti| tathā hi ‘‘yathāpi…pai॰… bahu’’nti (dha॰ pa॰ 53)? aittha puppharāsiṭṭhāniyatau manussūpapattisappurisūpanissaya-saddhammassavana- yaunisaumanasikāra-bhaugasampattiādidānādipuñ‍ñakiriyahaitusamudāyatau saubhāsugandhatādiguṇayaugatau mālāguḷasadisiyau pahūtā puñ‍ñakiriyā maritabbasabhāvatāya mac‍caina sattaina kattabbāti jautitattā  puppharāsimālāguḷāva upamā, taisaṃ upamākārau yathā-saddaina aniyamatau vuttauti ‘‘aivaṃ-saddau upamākāranigamanatthau’’ti  vatthuṃ yuttaṃ, sau pana upamākārau niyamiyamānau atthatau upamāva hautīti āha ‘‘upamāyaṃ āgatau’’ti| tathā ‘‘aivaṃ iminā ākāraina abhik‍kamitabba’’ntiādinā upadisiyamānāya samaṇasāruppāya ākappasampattiyā yau tattha upadisanākārau, sau atthatau upadaisau aivāti vuttaṃ, ‘‘aivaṃ tai…pai॰… upadaisai’’ti| tathā ‘‘aivamaitaṃ bhagavā, aivamaitaṃ sugatā’’ti aittha ca bhagavatā yathāvuttamatthaṃ aviparītatau jānantaihi kataṃ tattha saṃvij‍jamānaguṇānaṃ pakāraihi haṃsanaṃ udaggatākaraṇaṃ sampahaṃsanaṃ| yau tattha sampahaṃsanākārauti yaujaitabbaṃ|

aivamaivaṃ panāyanti aittha garahaṇākārauti yaujaitabbaṃ, sau ca garahaṇākārau vasalīti-ādikhuṃsanasaddasan‍nidhānatau idha aivaṃ-saddaina pakāsitauti viñ‍ñāyati| yathā caittha, aivaṃ upamākārādayaupi upamādivasaina vuttānaṃ puppharāsiādisaddānaṃ san‍nidhānatauti daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivaṃ, bhantaiti pana dhammassa sādhukaṃ savanamanasikārai san‍niyaujitaihi bhikkhūhi attanau tattha ṭhitabhāvassa paṭijānanavasaina vuttattā aittha aivaṃ-saddau ‘‘vacanasampaṭicchanatthau’’ti vuttau| taina aivaṃ, bhantaiti sādhu, bhantai, suṭṭhu, bhantaiti vuttaṃ hauti| aivañ‍ca vadaihīti yathāhaṃ vadāmi, aivaṃ samaṇaṃ ānandaṃ vadaihīti yau aivaṃ vadanākārau idāni vattabbau, sau aivaṃ-saddaina nidassīyatīti ‘‘nidassanatthau’’ti vuttauti| aivaṃ nauti aitthāpi naisaṃ yathāvuttadhammānaṃ ahitadukkhāvahabhāvai san‍niṭṭhānajananatthaṃ anumatigahaṇavasaina ‘‘saṃvattanti vā nau vā, kathaṃ vau aittha hautī’’ti pucchāya katāya ‘‘aivaṃ nau aittha hautī’’ti vuttattā tadākārasan‍niṭṭhānaṃ aivaṃ-saddaina vibhāvitanti viñ‍ñāyati| sau pana taisaṃ dhammānaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya saṃvattanākārau niyamiyamānau avadhāraṇatthau hautīti āha – ‘‘aivaṃ nau aittha hautītiādīsu avadhāraṇai’’ti|

nānānayanipuṇanti aikattanānattaabyāpāraaivaṃdhammatāsaṅkhātā, nandiyāvaṭṭatipukkhalasīhavik‍kīḷitaaṅkusadisālaucanasaṅkhātā vā ādhārādibhaidavasaina nānāvidhā nayā nānānayā| nayā vā pāḷigatiyau tā ca pañ‍ñattianupañ‍ñattiādivasaina saṃkilaisabhāgiyādilaukiyāditadubhayavaumissakādivasaina kusalādivasaina khandhādivasaina saṅgahādivasaina samayavimuttādivasaina ṭhapanādivasaina kusalamūlādivasaina tikappaṭṭhānādivasaina  ca nānappakārāti nānānayā, taihi nipuṇaṃ saṇhaṃ sukhumanti nānānayanipuṇaṃ| āsayauva ajjhāsayau, tai ca sassatādibhaidaina tattha ca apparajakkhatādibhaidaina anaikai, attajjhāsayādayau aiva vā samuṭṭhānaṃ uppattihaitu aitassāti anaikajjhāsayasamuṭṭhānaṃ| atthabyañ‍janasampan‍nanti atthabyañ‍janaparipuṇṇaṃ upanaitabbābhāvatau, saṅkāsanapakāsanavivaraṇavibhajanauttānīkaraṇapañ‍ñattivasaina chahi atthapadaihi akkharapadabyañ‍janākāraniruttiniddaisavasaina chahi byañ‍janapadaihi ca saman‍nāgatanti vā atthau daṭṭhabbau|

vividhapāṭihāriyanti aittha pāṭihāriyapadassa vacanatthaṃ (udā॰ aṭṭha॰ 1; itivu॰ aṭṭha॰ nidānavaṇṇanā; dha॰ sa॰ mūlaṭī॰ 2) ‘‘paṭipakkhaharaṇatau rāgādikilaisāpanayanatau pāṭihāriya’’nti vadanti| bhagavatau pana paṭipakkhā rāgādayau na santi yai haritabbā, puthuj‍janānampi vigatūpak‍kilaisai aṭṭhaguṇasaman‍nāgatai cittai hatapaṭipakkhai iddhividhaṃ pavattati, tasmā tattha pavattavauhāraina ca na sak‍kā idha ‘‘pāṭihāriya’’nti vattuṃ| sacai pana mahākāruṇikassa bhagavatau vainaiyyagatā ca kilaisā paṭipakkhā, taisaṃ haraṇatau ‘‘pāṭihāriya’’nti vuttaṃ, aivaṃ sati yuttamaitaṃ| atha vā bhagavatau ca sāsanassa ca paṭipakkhā titthiyā, taisaṃ haraṇatau pāṭihāriyaṃ| tai hi diṭṭhiharaṇavasaina diṭṭhippakāsanai asamatthabhāvaina ca iddhiādaisanānusāsanīhi haritā apanītā hauntīti| paṭīti vā ayaṃ saddau ‘‘pacchā’’ti aitassa atthaṃ baudhaiti ‘‘tasmiṃ paṭipaviṭṭhamhi, añ‍ñau āgañchi brāhmaṇau’’tiādīsu (su॰ ni॰ 985; cūḷani॰ vatthugāthā 4) viya, tasmā samāhitai cittai vigatūpak‍kilaisai katakic‍caina pacchāharitabbaṃ pavattaitabbanti paṭihāriyaṃ, attanau vā upak‍kilaisaisu catutthajjhānamaggaihi haritaisu pacchā haraṇaṃ paṭihāriyaṃ, iddhiādaisanānusāsaniyau ca vigatūpak‍kilaisaina katakic‍caina ca sattahitatthaṃ puna pavattaitabbā, haritaisu ca attanau upattilaisaisu parasattānaṃ upak‍kilaisaharaṇāni hauntīti paṭihāriyāni bhavanti, paṭihāriyamaiva pāṭihāriyaṃ| paṭihāriyai vā iddhiādaisanānusāsanisamudāyai bhavaṃ aikaikaṃ pāṭihāriyanti vuc‍cati| paṭihāriyaṃ vā catutthajjhānaṃ maggau ca paṭipakkhaharaṇatau, tattha jātaṃ, tasmiṃ vā nimittabhūtai, tatau vā āgatanti pāṭihāriyaṃ| tassa pana iddhiādibhaidaina visayabhaidaina ca bahuvidhassa  bhagavatau daisanāyaṃ labbhamānattā āha ‘‘vividhapāṭihāriya’’nti|

na añ‍ñathāti bhagavatau sammukhā sutākāratau na añ‍ñathāti atthau, na pana bhagavatau daisitākāratau| acintaiyyānubhāvā hi bhagavatau daisanā| aivañ‍ca katvā ‘‘sabbappakāraina kau samatthau viñ‍ñātu’’nti idaṃ vacanaṃ samatthitaṃ bhavati, dhāraṇabaladassanañ‍ca na virujjhati sutākāraavirajjhanassa adhippaitattā| na haittha atthantaratāparihārau dvin‍nampi atthānaṃ aikavisayattā| itarathā thairau bhagavatau daisanāya sabbathā paṭiggahaṇai samatthau asamatthau cāti āpaj‍jaiyyāti|

‘‘yau  parau na hauti, sau attā’’ti aivaṃ vuttāya niyakajjhattasaṅkhātāya sasantatiyā vattanatau tividhaupi mai-saddau kiñ‍cāpi aikasmiṃ aiva atthai dissati, karaṇasampadānasāminiddaisavasaina pana vij‍jamānaṃ bhaidaṃ sandhāyāha, ‘‘mai-saddau tīsu atthaisu dissatī’’ti|

kiñ‍cāpi upasaggau kiriyaṃ visaisaiti, jautakabhāvatau pana satipi tasmiṃ suta-saddau aiva taṃ taṃ attaṃ vadatīti anupasaggassa suta-saddassa atthuddhārai saupasaggassa gahaṇaṃ na virujjhatīti dassaintau ‘‘saupasaggau anupasaggau cā’’tiādimāha| assāti suta-saddassa| kammabhāvasādhanāni idha sutasaddai sambhavantīti vuttaṃ ‘‘upadhāritanti vā upadhāraṇanti vā atthau’’ti| mayāti atthai satīti yadā mai-saddassa kattuvasaina karaṇaniddaisau, tadāti atthau| mamāti atthai satīti yadā sambandhavasaina sāminiddaisau, tadā|

suta-saddasan‍nidhānai payuttaina aivaṃ-saddaina savanakiriyājautakaina bhavitabbanti vuttaṃ ‘‘aivanti sautaviñ‍ñāṇādiviñ‍ñāṇakic‍canidassana’’nti| ādi-saddaina sampaṭicchanādīnaṃ pañ‍cadvārikaviñ‍ñāṇānaṃ tadabhinīhaṭānañ‍ca manaudvārikaviñ‍ñāṇānaṃ gahaṇaṃ vaiditabbaṃ| sabbaisampi vākyānaṃ aivakāratthasahitattā ‘‘suta’’nti aitassa sutamaivāti ayamatthau labbhatīti āha ‘‘assavanabhāvapaṭikkhaipatau’’ti| aitaina avadhāraṇaina nirāsaṅkataṃ dassaiti| yathā ca sutaṃ sutamaivāti niyamaitabbaṃ, taṃ sammā sutaṃ hautīti āha ‘‘anūnānadhikāviparītaggahaṇanidassana’’nti| atha vā saddantaratthāpauhanavasaina  saddau atthaṃ vadatīti sutanti asutaṃ na hautīti ayamaitassa atthauti vuttaṃ ‘‘assavanabhāvapaṭikkhaipatau’’ti| iminā diṭṭhādivinivattanaṃ karauti|

idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti – na idaṃ mayā diṭṭhaṃ, na sayambhūñāṇaina sacchikataṃ, atha khau sutaṃ, tañ‍ca khau sammadaivāti| tainaivāha ‘‘anūnānadhikāviparītaggahaṇanidassana’’nti| avadhāraṇatthai vā aivaṃ-saddai ayamatthayaujanā karīyatīti tadapaikkhassa suta-saddassa ayamatthau vuttau ‘‘assavanabhāvapaṭikkhaipatau’’ti| tainaivāha ‘‘anūnānadhikāviparītaggahaṇanidassana’’nti| savanasaddau caittha kammatthau vaiditabbau ‘‘suyyatī’’ti|

aivaṃ savanahaitusavanavisaisavasaina padattayassa aikaina pakāraina atthayaujanaṃ dassaitvā idāni pakārantaraihi taṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘tathā aiva’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| tattha tassāti yā sā bhagavatau sammukhā  dhammassavanākāraina pavattā manaudvāraviñ‍ñāṇavīthi, tassā| sā hi nānappakāraina ārammaṇai pavattaituṃ samatthā| tathā ca vuttaṃ ‘‘sautadvārānusārainā’’ti| nānappakāraināti vakkhamānānaṃ anaikavihitānaṃ byañ‍janatthaggahaṇānaṃ nānākāraina| aitaina imissā yaujanāya ākāratthau aivaṃ-saddau gahitauti dīpaiti| pavattibhāvappakāsananti pavattiyā atthitāpakāsanaṃ| sutanti dhammappakāsananti yasmiṃ ārammaṇai vuttappakārā viñ‍ñāṇavīthi nānappakāraina pavattā, tassa dhammattā vuttaṃ, na suta-saddassa dhammatthattā| vuttassaivatthassa pākaṭīkaraṇaṃ ‘‘ayaṃ haitthā’’tiādi| tattha viñ‍ñāṇavīthiyāti karaṇatthai karaṇavacanaṃ, mayāti kattuatthai|

aivanti niddisitabbappakāsananti nidassanatthamaivaṃ saddaṃ gahaitvā vuttaṃ nidassaitabbassa niddisitabbattābhāvābhāvatau| taina aivaṃ-saddaina sakalampi suttaṃ pac‍cāmaṭṭhanti dassaiti| suta-saddassa kiriyāsaddattā savanakiriyāya ca sādhāraṇaviñ‍ñāṇappabandhapaṭibaddhattā tattha ca puggalavauhārauti vuttaṃ ‘‘sutanti puggalakic‍cappakāsana’’nti| na hi puggalavauhārarahitai dhammappabandhai savanakiriyā labbhatīti|

yassa cittasantānassātiādipi ākāratthamaiva aivaṃ-saddaṃ gahaitvā purimayaujanāya añ‍ñathā atthayaujanaṃ dassaituṃ vuttaṃ| tattha ākārapañ‍ñattīti upādāpañ‍ñatti aiva dhammānaṃ pavattiākārupādānavasaina tathā  vuttā| sutanti visayaniddaisauti sautabbabhūtau dhammau savanakiriyākattupuggalassa savanakiriyāvasaina pavattiṭṭhānanti katvā vuttaṃ| cittasantānavinimuttassa paramatthatau kassaci kattu abhāvaipi saddavauhāraina buddhiparikappitabhaidavacanicchāya cittasantānatau añ‍ñaṃ viya taṃsamaṅgiṃ katvā vuttaṃ ‘‘cittasantānaina taṃsamaṅgīnau’’ti| savanakiriyāvisayaupi sautabbadhammau savanakiriyāvasaina pavattacittasantānassa idha paramatthatau kattubhāvatau, savanavasaina cittappavattiyā aiva vā savanakiriyābhāvatau taṃkiriyākattu ca visayau hautīti vuttaṃ ‘‘taṃsamaṅgīnau kattuvisayai’’ti| sutākārassa ca thairassa sammānicchitabhāvatau āha ‘‘gahaṇasan‍niṭṭhāna’’nti| aitaina vā avadhāraṇatthaṃ aivaṃ-saddaṃ gahaitvā ayamatthayaujanā katāti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

pubbai sutānaṃ nānāvihitānaṃ suttasaṅkhātānaṃ atthabyañ‍janānaṃ upadhāritarūpassa ākārassa nidassanassa, avadhāraṇassa vā pakāsanasabhāvau aivaṃ-saddauti tadākārādiupadhāraṇassa puggalapañ‍ñattiyā upādānabhūtadhammappabandhabyāpāratāya vuttaṃ ‘‘aivanti puggalakic‍caniddaisau’’ti| savanakiriyā pana puggalavādinaupi viñ‍ñāṇanirapaikkhā natthīti visaisatau viñ‍ñāṇabyāpārauti āha  ‘‘sutanti viñ‍ñāṇakic‍caniddaisau’’ti| maiti saddappavattiyā aikantainaiva sattavisayattā viñ‍ñāṇakic‍cassa ca tatthaiva samaudahitabbatau ‘‘maiti ubhayakic‍cayuttapuggalaniddaisau’’ti vuttaṃ| avij‍jamānapañ‍ñattivij‍jamānapañ‍ñattisabhāvā yathāk‍kamaṃ aivaṃ-saddasuta-saddānaṃ atthāti tai tathārūpa-pañ‍ñatti-upādānabhūta-dhammappabandhabyāpārabhāvaina dassaintau āha – ‘‘aivanti puggalakic‍caniddaisau, sutanti viñ‍ñāṇakic‍caniddaisau’’ti| aittha ca karaṇakiriyākattukamma-visaisappakāsanavasaina puggalabyāpāravisaya-puggalabyāpāranidassanavasaina gahaṇākāragāhakatabbisayavisaisaniddaisavasaina kattukaraṇabyāpāra-kattuniddaisavasaina ca dutiyādayau catassau atthayaujanā dassitāti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

sabbassapi saddādhigamanīyassa atthassa pañ‍ñattimukhainaiva paṭipaj‍jitabbattā sabbapañ‍ñattīnañ‍ca vij‍jamānādivasaina chasu pañ‍ñattibhaidaisu antaugadhattā taisu ‘‘aiva’’ntiādīnaṃ pañ‍ñattīnaṃ sarūpaṃ niddhāraintau āha ‘‘aivanti ca maiti cā’’tiādi| tattha aivanti ca maiti ca vuc‍camānassa atthassa ākārādinau  dhammānaṃ asalakkhaṇabhāvatau avij‍jamānapañ‍ñattibhāvauti āha ‘‘sac‍cikaṭṭhaparamatthavasaina avij‍jamānapañ‍ñattī’’ti| tattha sac‍cikaṭṭhaparamatthavasaināti bhūtatthauttamatthavasaina| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti – yau māyāmarīciādayau viya abhūtatthau, anussavādīhi gahaitabbau viya anuttamatthau ca na hauti, sau rūpasaddādisabhāvau, ruppanānubhavanādisabhāvau vā atthau sac‍cikaṭṭhau paramatthau cāti vuc‍cati, na tathā aivaṃ maiti padānaṃ atthauti| aitamaivatthaṃ pākaṭataraṃ kātuṃ ‘‘kiñhaitthata’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| sutanti pana saddāyatanaṃ sandhāyāha ‘‘vij‍jamānapañ‍ñattī’’ti| tainaiva hi ‘‘yañhi taṃ aittha sautaina upaladdha’’nti vuttaṃ| sautadvārānusāraina upaladdhanti pana vuttai atthabyañ‍janādi sabbaṃ labbhatīti| taṃ taṃ upādāya vattabbatauti sautapathamāgatai dhammai upādāya taisaṃ upadhāritākārādinau pac‍cāmasanavasaina aivanti, sasantatipariyāpan‍nai khandhai upādāya maiti vattabbattāti atthau| diṭṭhādisabhāvarahitai saddāyatanai pavattamānaupi sutavauhārau ‘‘dutiyaṃ tatiya’’ntiādikau viya paṭhamādīni diṭṭhamutaviñ‍ñātai apaikkhitvā pavattauti āha ‘‘diṭṭhādīni upanidhāya vattabbatau’’ti| asutaṃ na hautīti hi sutanti pakāsitauyamatthauti|

attanā paṭividdhā suttassa pakāravisaisā aivanti thairaina pac‍cāmaṭṭhāti āha ‘‘asammauhaṃ dīpaitī’’ti| nānappakārapaṭivaidhasamatthau hautīti aitaina vakkhamānassa suttassa nānappakārataṃ duppaṭivijjhatañ‍ca dassaiti| sutassa asammausaṃ dīpaitīti sutākārassa yāthāvatau dassiyamānattā vuttaṃ| asammauhaināti sammauhābhāvaina, pañ‍ñāya aiva vā savanakālasambhūtāya taduttarakālapañ‍ñāsiddhi| aivaṃ asammausaināti aitthāpi vattabbaṃ| byañ‍janānaṃ paṭivijjhitabbau ākārau nātigambhīrau yathāsutadhāraṇamaiva tattha karaṇīyanti satiyā byāpārau adhikau, pañ‍ñā tattha guṇībhūtāti vuttaṃ ‘‘pañ‍ñāpubbaṅgamāyā’’tiādi pañ‍ñāya pubbaṅgamāti katvā| pubbaṅgamatā caittha padhānatā ‘‘manaupubbaṅgamā’’tiādīsu viya| pubbaṅgamatāya vā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇādīsu āvaj‍janādīnaṃ viya appadhānattai pañ‍ñā pubbaṅgamā aitissāti ayampi atthau yuj‍jati, aivaṃ sati pubbaṅgamāyāti aitthāpi vuttavipariyāyaina yathāsambhavaṃ atthau vaiditabbau| atthabyañ‍janasampan‍nassāti atthabyañ‍janaparipuṇṇassa, saṅkāsana-pakāsana-vivaraṇa-vibhajana-uttānīkaraṇapañ‍ñattivasaina chahi atthapadaihi, akkharapadabyañ‍janākāraniruttiniddaisavasaina chahi byañ‍janapadaihi ca saman‍nāgatassāti vā atthau daṭṭhabbau|

yaunisau manasikāraṃ dīpaiti aivaṃ-saddaina vuc‍camānānaṃ ākāranidassanāvadhāraṇatthānaṃ aviparītasaddhammavisayattāti adhippāyau| avikkhaipaṃ dīpaitīti ‘‘aughataraṇasuttaṃ kattha bhāsita’’ntiādipucchāvasaina pakaraṇappavattassa vakkhamānassa suttassa savanaṃ samādhānamantaraina na sambhavatīti katvā vuttaṃ| vikkhittacittassātiādi tassaivatthassa samatthanavasaina vuttaṃ| sabbasampattiyāti atthabyañ‍janadaisaka-payaujanādisampattiyā| aviparītasaddhammavisayaihi viya ākāranidassanāvadhāraṇatthaihi yaunisaumanasikārassa, saddhammassavanaina viya ca avikkhaipassa yathā yaunisaumanasikāraina phalabhūtaina attasammāpaṇidhipubbaikatapuñ‍ñatānaṃ siddhi vuttā tadavinābhāvatau, aivaṃ avikkhaipainaiva phalabhūtaina kāraṇabhūtānaṃ saddhammassavanasappurisūpanissayānaṃ siddhi dassaitabbā siyā assutavatau sappurisūpanissayarahitassa ca tadabhāvatau| ‘‘na hi vikkhittacittau’’tiādinā samatthanavacanaina pana avikkhaipaina kāraṇabhūtaina sappurisūpanissayaina ca phalabhūtassa saddhammassavanassa siddhi dassitā| ayaṃ panaittha adhippāyau yuttau siyā, saddhammassavanasappurisūpanissayā na aikantaina avikkhaipassa kāraṇaṃ bāhiraṅgattā| avikkhaipau pana sappurisūpanissayau viya saddhammassavanassa aikantakāraṇanti, aivampi avikkhaipaina sappurisūpanissayasiddhijautanā na samatthitāva, nau na samatthitā vikkhittacittānaṃ sappurisapayirūpāsanābhāvassa atthasiddhitau| aittha ca purimaṃ phalaina kāraṇassa siddhidassanaṃ nadīpūraina viya upari vuṭṭhisabbhāvassa| dutiyaṃ kāraṇaina phalassa siddhidassanaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ aikantavassinā viya maighavuṭṭhānaina vuṭṭhippavattiyā|

bhagavatau vacanassa atthabyañ‍janapabhaida-paricchaidavasaina sakalasāsanasampatti-augāhanākārau niravasaisaparahita-pāripūrikāraṇanti vuttaṃ ‘‘aivaṃ bhaddakau ākārau’’ti| yasmā na hautīti sambandhau| pacchimacak‍kadvayasampattinti attasammāpaṇidhi-pubbaikatapuñ‍ñatā-saṅkhātaṃ guṇadvayaṃ| aparāparavuttiyā caittha cak‍kabhāvau, caranti aitaihi sattā, sampattibhavaisūti vā| yai sandhāya vuttaṃ ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, cak‍kāni, yaihi saman‍nāgatānaṃ daivamanussānaṃ catucak‍kaṃ vattatī’’tiādi (a॰ ni॰ 4.31)| purimapacchimabhāvau caittha daisanak‍kamavasaina daṭṭhabbau| pacchimacak‍kadvayasiddhiyāti pacchimacak‍kadvayassa ca atthitāya| sammāpaṇihitattau pubbai ca katapuñ‍ñau suddhāsayau hauti tadasuddhihaitūnaṃ kilaisānaṃ dūrībhāvatauti āha ‘‘āsayasuddhi siddhā hautī’’ti| tathā hi vuttaṃ – ‘‘sammāpaṇihitaṃ cittaṃ, saiyyasau naṃ tatau karai’’ti (dha॰ pa॰ 43) ‘‘katapuñ‍ñausi, tvaṃ ānanda, padhānamanuyuñ‍ja, khippaṃ hauhisi anāsavau’’ti (dī॰ ni॰ 2.207) ca| tainaivāha ‘‘āsayasuddhiyā adhigamabyattisiddhī’’ti| payaugasuddhiyāti yaunisaumanasikārapubbaṅgamassa dhammassavanapayaugassa visadabhāvaina| tathā cāha ‘‘āgamabyattisiddhī’’ti, sabbassa vā kāyavacīpayaugassa niddausabhāvaina| parisuddhakāyavacīpayaugau hi vippaṭisārābhāvatau avikkhittacittau pariyattiyaṃ visāradau hautīti|

nānappakārapaṭivaidhadīpakainātiādinā atthabyañ‍janaisu thairassa aivaṃ-saddasuta-saddānaṃ asammauhāsammausadīpanatau catupaṭisambhidāvasaina atthayaujanaṃ dassaiti| tattha sautabbabhaidapaṭivaidhadīpakaināti aitaina ayaṃ suta-saddau aivaṃ-saddasan‍nidhānatau, vakkhamānāpaikkhāya vā sāmañ‍ñainaiva sautabbadhammavisaisaṃ āmasatīti dīpaiti| manaudiṭṭhīhi pariyattidhammānaṃ anupaikkhanasuppaṭivaidhā visaisatau manasikārapaṭibaddhāti tai vuttanayaina yaunisaumanasikāradīpakaina aivaṃ saddaina yaujaitvā, savanadhāraṇavacīparicayā pariyattidhammānaṃ visaisaina sautāvadhānapaṭibaddhāti tai vuttanayaina avikkhaipadīpakaina suta-saddaina yaujaitvā dassaintau sāsanasampattiyā dhammassavanai ussāhaṃ janaiti| tattha dhammāti pariyattidhammā| manasā anupaikkhitāti ‘‘idha sīlaṃ kathitaṃ, idha samādhi, idha pañ‍ñā, aittakā aittha anusandhiyau’’tiādinā nayaina manasā anupaikkhitā| diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhāti nijjhānakkhanti bhūtāya, ñātapariñ‍ñāsaṅkhātāya vā diṭṭhiyā tattha tattha vuttarūpārūpadhammai ‘‘iti rūpaṃ, aittakaṃ rūpa’’ntiādinā suṭṭhu vavatthapaitvā paṭividdhā|

sakalaina vacanaināti pubbai tīhi padaihi visuṃ visuṃ yaujitattā vuttaṃ| attanau adahantauti ‘‘mamaida’’nti attani aṭṭhapaintau| bhummatthai caitaṃ  sāmivacanaṃ| asappurisabhūminti akatañ‍ñutaṃ| ‘‘idhaikac‍cau pāpabhikkhu tathāgatappavaiditaṃ dhammavinayaṃ pariyāpuṇitvā attanau dahatī’’ti (pārā॰ 195) aivaṃ vuttaṃ anariyavauhārāvatthaṃ, sā aiva anariyavauhārāvatthā asaddhammau| nanu ca ānandatthairassa ‘‘mamaidaṃ vacana’’nti adhimānassa, mahākassapatthairādīnañ‍ca tadāsaṅkāya abhāvatau asappurisabhūmi-samatik‍kamādivacanaṃ niratthakanti? nayidamaivaṃ| ‘‘aivaṃ mai suta’’nti vadantaina ayampi atthau vibhāvitauti dassanatau| kaici pana ‘‘daivatānaṃ parivitak‍kāpaikkhaṃ tathāvacananti aidisī caudanā anavakāsā’’ti vadanti| tasmiṃ kira khaṇai aikac‍cānaṃ daivatānaṃ aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi ‘‘bhagavā ca parinibbutau, ayañ‍ca āyasmā daisanākusalau idāni dhammaṃ daisaiti, sakyakulappasutau tathāgatassa bhātā cūḷapituputtau| kiṃ nu khau sayaṃ sacchikataṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti? udāhu bhagavatau aiva vacanaṃ yathāsuta’’nti, aivaṃ tadāsaṅkitappakāratau asappurisabhūmisamauk‍kamāditau atik‍kamādi vibhāvitanti| appaitīti nidassaiti| diṭṭhadhammika-samparāyika-paramatthaisu yathārahaṃ sattai naitīti naitti, dhammau aiva naitti dhammanaitti|

daḷhataraniviṭṭhā vicikicchā kaṅkhā, nātisaṃsappanā matibhaidamattā vimati| assaddhiyaṃ vināsaitīti bhagavatā bhāsitattā sammukhāvassa paṭiggahitattā khalitaduruttādigahaṇadausābhāvatau ca| aittha ca paṭhamādayau tissau atthayaujanā ākārādiatthaisu aggahitavisaisamaiva aivaṃ-saddaṃ gahaitvā dassitā, tatau parā catassau ākāratthamaiva aivaṃ-saddaṃ gahaitvā vibhāvitā| pacchimā pana tissau yathāk‍kamaṃ ākāratthaṃ nidassanatthaṃ avadhāraṇatthañ‍ca aivaṃ-saddaṃ gahaitvā yaujitāti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

aika-saddau añ‍ñasaiṭṭhaasahāyasaṅkhayādīsu dissati| tathā haisa ‘‘sassatau attā ca laukau ca, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ñanti itthaikai abhivadantī’’tiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 3.27) añ‍ñatthai dissati| ‘‘caitasau aikaudibhāva’’ntiādīsu (dī॰ ni॰ 1.228; pārā॰ 11) saiṭṭhai, ‘‘aikau vūpakaṭṭhau’’tiādīsu (dī॰ ni॰ 1.405) asahāyai, ‘‘aikauva khau, bhikkhavai, khaṇau ca samayau ca brahmacariyavāsāyā’’tiādīsu (a॰ ni॰ 8.29) saṅkhayāyaṃ| idhāpi saṅkhayāyanti dassaintau āha ‘‘aikanti  gaṇanaparicchaidaniddaisau’’ti| kālañ‍ca samayañ‍cāti yuttakālañ‍ca pac‍cayasāmaggiñ‍ca| khaṇauti aukāsau| tathāgatuppādādikau hi maggabrahmacariyassa aukāsau tappac‍cayassa paṭilābhahaituttā| khaṇau aiva ca samayau, yau khaṇauti ca samayauti ca vuc‍cati, sau aikau aivāti hi atthau| mahāsamayauti mahāsamūhau| samayaupi khauti sikkhāpadapūraṇassa haitupi| samayappavādakaiti diṭṭhippavādakai| tattha hi nisin‍nā titthiyā attanau attanau samayaṃ pavadanti| atthābhisamayāti hitapaṭilābhā| abhisamaitabbauti abhisamayau, abhisamayau atthau abhisamayatthauti pīḷanādīni abhisamaitabbabhāvaina aikībhāvaṃ upanaitvā  vuttāni| abhisamayassa vā paṭivaidhassa visayabhūtau atthau abhisamayatthauti tānaiva tathā aikattaina vuttāni| tattha pīḷanaṃ dukkhasac‍cassa taṃsamaṅginau hiṃsanaṃ avipphārikatākaraṇaṃ, santāpau dukkhadukkhatādivasaina santāpanaṃ paridahanaṃ|

tattha sahakārikāraṇaṃ san‍nijjhaṃ samaiti samavaitīti samayau, samavāyau| samaiti samāgacchati aittha maggabrahmacariyaṃ tadādhārapuggalaihīti samayau, khaṇau| samaiti aittha, aitaina vā saṃgacchati sattau, sabhāvadhammau vā sahajātādīhi, uppādādīhi vāti samayau, kālau| dhammappavattimattatāya atthatau abhūtaupi hi kālau dhammappavattiyā adhikaraṇaṃ karaṇaṃ viya ca parikappanāmattasiddhaina rūpaina vauharīyatīti| samaṃ, saha vā avayavānaṃ ayanaṃ pavatti avaṭṭhānanti samayau, samūhau yathā ‘‘samudāyau’’ti| avayavasahāvaṭṭhānamaiva hi samūhauti| avasaisapac‍cayānaṃ samāgamai aiti phalaṃ aitasmā uppaj‍jati pavattati cāti samayau, haitu yathā ‘‘samudayau’’ti| samaiti saṃyaujanabhāvatau sambandhau aiti attanau visayai pavattati, daḷhaggahaṇabhāvatau vā saṃyuttā ayanti pavattanti sattā yathābhinivaisaṃ aitaināti samayau, diṭṭhi| diṭṭhisaṃyaujanaina hi sattā ativiya bajjhantīti| samiti saṃgati samaudhānanti samayau, paṭilābhau| samassa niraudhassa yānaṃ, sammā vā yānaṃ apagamau apavatti samayau, pahānaṃ| abhimukhaṃ ñāṇaina sammā aitabbau adhigantabbauti abhisamayau, dhammānaṃ aviparītau sabhāvau| abhimukhabhāvaina sammā aiti gacchati bujjhatīti abhisamayau, dhammānaṃ aviparītasabhāvāvabaudhau| aivaṃ tasmiṃ tasmiṃ atthai samayasaddappavatti vaiditabbā| samayasaddassa atthuddhārai abhisamayasaddassa udāharaṇaṃ vuttanayainaiva vaiditabbaṃ| assāti samayasaddassa| kālau atthau samavāyādīnaṃ atthānaṃ idha asambhavatau daisadaisakaparisānaṃ viya suttassa nidānabhāvaina kālassa apadisitabbatau ca|

kasmā panaittha aniyamitavasainaiva kālau niddiṭṭhau? na utusaṃvaccharādivasaina niyamitauti āha ‘‘tattha kiñ‍cāpī’’tiādi| utusaṃvaccharādivasaina niyamaṃ akatvā samayasaddassa vacanai ayampi guṇau laddhau hautīti dassaintau ‘‘yai vā imai’’tiādimāha| sāmañ‍ñajautanā hi visaisai avatiṭṭhatīti| tattha diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārasamayau daivasikaṃ jhānaphalasamāpattīhi vītināmanakālau, visaisatau sattasattāhāni| suppakāsāti dasasahassilaukadhātupakampana-aubhāsapātubhāvādīhi pākaṭā| yathāvuttappabhaidaisu aiva samayaisu aikadaisaṃ pakārantaraihi saṅgahaitvā dassaituṃ ‘‘yau cāya’’ntiādimāha| tathā hi ñāṇakic‍casamayau, attahitapaṭipattisamayau  ca abhisambaudhisamayau, ariyatuṇhībhāvasamayau, diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārasamayau, karuṇākic‍caparahitapaṭipattidhammīkathāsamayā, daisanāsamayau aiva|

karaṇavacanaina niddaisau katauti sambandhau| tatthāti abhidhammatadañ‍ñasuttapadavinayaisu| tathāti bhummakaraṇaihi| adhikaraṇatthau ādhāratthau| bhāvau nāma kiriyā, tāya kiriyantaralakkhaṇaṃ bhāvainabhāvalakkhaṇaṃ| tattha yathā kālau sabhāvadhammaparicchin‍nau sayaṃ paramatthatau avij‍jamānaupi ādhārabhāvaina pañ‍ñātau taṅkhaṇappavattānaṃ tatau pubbai paratau ca abhāvatau ‘‘pubbaṇhai jātau, sāyanhai gacchatī’’ti ca ādīsu, samūhau ca avayavavinimuttau visuṃ avij‍jamānaupi kappanāmattasiddhau avayavānaṃ ādhārabhāvaina pañ‍ñāpīyati yathā ‘‘rukkhai sākhā, yavarāsiyaṃ sambhūtau’’tiādīsu, aivaṃ idhāpīti dassaintau āha ‘‘adhikaraṇañhi…pai॰… dhammāna’’nti| yasmiṃ kālai, dhammapuñ‍jai vā kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti, tasmiṃ aiva kālai dhammapuñ‍jai ca phassādayaupi hauntīti ayaṃ hi tattha atthau| yathā ca ‘‘gāvīsu duyhamānāsu gatau, duddhāsu āgatau’’ti dauhanakiriyāya gamanakiriyā lakkhīyati, aivaṃ idhāpi yasmiṃ samayai, tasmiṃ samayaiti ca vuttai  ‘‘satī’’ti ayamatthau viñ‍ñāyamānau aiva hauti padatthassa sattāvirahābhāvatauti samayassa sattākiriyāya cittassa uppādakiriyā phassādibhavanakiriyā ca lakkhīyati| yasmiṃ samayaiti yasmiṃ navamai khaṇai, yasmiṃ yaunisaumanasikārādihaitumhi, pac‍cayasamavāyai vā sati kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti, tasmiṃyaiva khaṇai, haitumhi, pac‍cayasamavāyai vā phassādayaupi hauntīti ubhayattha samayasaddai bhummaniddaisau katau lakkhaṇabhūtabhāvayuttauti dassaintau āha ‘‘khaṇa…pai॰… lakkhīyatī’’ti|

haituatthau karaṇatthau ca sambhavati ‘‘an‍naina vasati, vij‍jāya vasati, pharasunā chindati, kudālaina khaṇatī’’tiādīsu viya| vītik‍kamañhi sutvā bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā autiṇṇavatthukaṃ puggalaṃ paṭipucchitvā vigarahitvā ca taṃ taṃ vatthuautiṇṇakālaṃ anatik‍kamitvā tainaiva kālaina sikkhāpadāni pañ‍ñapaintau bhagavā viharati sikkhāpadapañ‍ñattihaituñ‍ca apaikkhamānau tatiyapārājikādīsu viya|

ac‍cantamaiva ārambhatau paṭṭhāya yāva daisanāniṭṭhānaṃ| parahitapaṭipattisaṅkhātaina karuṇāvihāraina| tadatthajautanatthanti ac‍cantasaṃyaugatthajautanatthaṃ| upayauganiddaisau katau yathā ‘‘māsaṃ sajjhāyatī’’ti| paurāṇāti aṭṭhakathācariyā| abhilāpamattabhaidauti vacanamattaina visaisau| taina suttavinayaisu vibhattibyattayau katauti dassaiti|

saiṭṭhanti saiṭṭhavācakaṃ vacanaṃ ‘‘saiṭṭha’’nti vuttaṃ saiṭṭhaguṇasahacaraṇatau, tathā ‘‘uttama’’nti aitthāpi| gāravayuttauti garubhāvayuttau garuguṇayaugatau, garukaraṇārahatāya vā gāravayuttau| vuttauyaiva, na pana idha vattabbau visuddhimaggassa imissā aṭṭhakathāya aikadaisabhāvatauti adhippāyau|

aparau nayau (itivu॰ aṭṭha॰ nidānavaṇṇanā; sārattha॰ ṭī॰ 1.1.vairañ‍jakaṇḍavaṇṇanā; visuddhi॰ mahāṭī॰ 1.144) – bhāgavāti bhagavā, bhatavāti bhagavā, bhāgai vanīti bhagavā, bhagai vanīti bhagavā, bhattavāti bhagavā, bhagai vamīti bhagavā, bhāgai vamīti bhagavā|

‘‘bhāgavā  bhatavā bhāgai, bhagai ca vani bhattavā|

bhagai vami tathā bhāgai, vamīti bhagavā jinau’’||

tattha kathaṃ bhāgavāti bhagavā? yai tai sīlādayau dhammakkhandhā guṇabhāgā guṇakauṭṭhāsā, tai anañ‍ñasādhāraṇā niratisayā tathāgatai atthi upalabbhanti| tathā hissa sīlaṃ samādhi pañ‍ñā vimutti vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ, hirī auttappaṃ, saddhā vīriyaṃ, sati sampajañ‍ñaṃ, sīlavisuddhi diṭṭhivisuddhi, samathau vipassanā, tīṇi kusalamūlāni, tīṇi sucaritāni, tayau sammāvitak‍kā, tissau anavaj‍jasañ‍ñā, tissau dhātuyau, cattārau satipaṭṭhānā, cattārau sammappadhānā, cattārau iddhipādā, cattārau ariyamaggā, cattāri ariyaphalāni, catassau paṭisambhidā, catuyauniparicchaidakañāṇāni, cattārau ariyavaṃsā, cattāri vaisāraj‍jañāṇāni, pañ‍ca padhāniyaṅgāni, pañ‍caṅgikau sammāsamādhi, pañ‍cañāṇikau sammāsamādhi, pañ‍cindriyāni, pañ‍ca balāni, pañ‍ca nissāraṇīyā dhātuyau, pañ‍ca vimuttāyatanañāṇāni, pañ‍ca vimuttiparipācanīyā sañ‍ñā, cha anussatiṭṭhānāni, cha gāravā, cha nissāraṇīyā dhātuyau, cha satatavihārā, cha anuttariyāni, cha nibbaidhabhāgiyā sañ‍ñā, cha abhiñ‍ñā, cha asādhāraṇañāṇāni, satta aparihāniyā dhammā, satta ariyadhammā, satta ariyadhanāni, satta baujjhaṅgā, satta sappurisadhammā, satta nij‍jaravatthūni, satta sañ‍ñā, sattadakkhiṇaiyyapuggaladaisanā, sattakhīṇāsavabaladaisanā, aṭṭhapañ‍ñāpaṭilābhahaitudaisanā aṭṭha sammattāni, aṭṭhalaukadhammātik‍kamau, aṭṭha  ārambhavatthūni, aṭṭhaakkhaṇadaisanā, aṭṭha mahāpurisavitak‍kā, aṭṭhaabhibhāyatanadaisanā, aṭṭha vimaukkhā, nava yaunisaumanasikāramūlakā dhammā, nava pārisuddhipadhāniyaṅgāni, navasattāvāsadaisanā, nava āghātapaṭivinayā, nava sañ‍ñā, navanānattā, nava anupubbavihārā, dasa nāthakaraṇā dhammā, dasa kasiṇāyatanāni, dasa kusalakammapathā, dasa sammattāni, dasa ariyavāsā, dasa asaikkhadhammā, dasa tathāgatabalāni, aikādasa maittānisaṃsā, dvādasa dhammācak‍kākārā, tairasa dhutaguṇā, cuddasa buddhañāṇāni, pañ‍cadasa vimuttiparipācanīyā dhammā, sauḷasavidhā ānāpānassati, sauḷasa aparantapanīyā dhammā, aṭṭhārasa buddhadhammā, aikūnavīsati pac‍cavaikkhaṇañāṇāni, catucattālīsa ñāṇavatthūni, pañ‍ñāsa udayabbayañāṇāni, paraupañ‍ñāsa kusalā dhammā, sattasattati ñāṇavatthūni, catuvīsatikauṭisatasahassasaṅkhasamāpattisañ‍cārimahāvajirañāṇaṃ, anantanayasamantapaṭṭhāna-pavicaya-pac‍cavaikkhaṇadaisanāñāṇāni, tathā anantāsu laukadhātūsu anantānaṃ sattānaṃ āsayādivibhāvanañāṇāni cāti aivamādayau anantāparimāṇabhaidā anañ‍ñasādhāraṇā niratisayā guṇabhāgā guṇakauṭṭhāsā saṃvij‍janti upalabbhanti, tasmā yathāvuttavibhāgā guṇabhāgā assa atthīti ‘‘bhāgavā’’ti vattabbai ākārassa rassattaṃ katvā ‘‘bhagavā’’ti vuttau| aivaṃ tāva bhāgavāti bhagavā|

‘‘yasmā sīlādayau sabbai, guṇabhāgā asaisatau|

vij‍janti sugatai tasmā, bhagavāti pavuc‍cati’’||

kathaṃ bhatavāti bhagavā? yai tai sabbalaukahitāya ussuk‍kamāpan‍naihi manussattādikai aṭṭha dhammai samaudhānaitvā sammāsambaudhiyā katamahābhinīhāraihi mahābaudhisattaihi paripūritabbā dānapāramī, sīla, naikkhamma, pañ‍ñā, vīriya, khanti, sac‍ca, adhiṭṭhāna, maittā, upaikkhāpāramīti dasa pāramiyau, dasa upapāramiyau, dasa paramatthapāramiyauti samatiṃsa pāramiyau, dānādīni cattāri saṅgahavatthūni, sac‍cādīni cattāri adhiṭṭhānāni, aṅgaparic‍cāgau, jīvita, raj‍ja, putta, dāraparic‍cāgauti pañ‍ca mahāparic‍cāgā, pubbayaugau, pubbacariyā, dhammakkhānaṃ, ñātatthacariyā, laukatthacariyā, buddhicariyāti aivamādayau, saṅkhaipatau vā sabbai puñ‍ñañāṇasambhārā buddhakaradhammā, tai mahābhinīhāratau paṭṭhāya kappānaṃ satasahassādhikāni cattāri asaṅkhyaiyyāni yathā hānabhāgiyā saṃkilaisabhāgiyā ṭhitibhāgiyā vā na haunti, atha khau uttaruttari visaisabhāgiyāva haunti, aivaṃ sak‍kac‍caṃ nirantaraṃ anavasaisatau bhatā sambhatā assa atthīti ‘‘bhatavā’’ti vattabbai ‘‘bhagavā’’ti vuttau niruttinayaina ta-kārassa ga-kāraṃ katvā| atha vā bhatavāti taiyaiva yathāvuttai buddhakaradhammai vuttanayainaiva bhari, sambhari, paripūraisīti atthau| aivampi bhatavāti bhagavā|

‘‘sammāsambaudhiyā sabbai, dānapāramiādikai|

sambhārai bhatavā nāthau, tasmāpi bhagavā matau’’||

kathaṃ bhāgai vanīti bhagavā? yai tai catuvīsatikauṭisatasahassasaṅkhā daivasikaṃ vaḷañ‍janakasamāpattibhāgā, tai anavasaisatau laukahitatthaṃ  attanau ca diṭṭhadhammasukhavihāratthaṃ nic‍cakappaṃ vani, bhaji, saivi, bahulamakāsīti bhāgai vanīti bhagavā| atha vā abhiñ‍ñaiyyadhammaisu kusalādīsu khandhādīsu ca yai tai pariñ‍ñaiyyādivasaina saṅkhaipatau vā catubbidhā abhisamayabhāgā, vitthāratau pana ‘‘cakkhu pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ sautaṃ…pai॰… jarāmaraṇaṃ pariñ‍ñaiyya’’ntiādinā (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.21) anaikai pariñ‍ñaiyyabhāgā, ‘‘cakkhussa samudayau pahātabbau…pai॰… jarāmaraṇassa samudayau pahātabbau’’tiādinā pahātabbabhāgā, ‘‘cakkhussa niraudhau…pai॰… jarāmaraṇassa niraudhau sacchikātabbau’’tiādinā sacchikātabbabhāgā, ‘‘cakkhuniraudhagāminīpaṭipadā’’tiādinā, ‘‘cattārau satipaṭṭhānā’’tiādinā ca anaikabhaidā bhāvaitabbabhāgā ca dhammā, tai sabbai vani, bhaji, yathārahaṃ gaucarabhāvanāsaivanānaṃ vasaina saivi| aivampi bhāgai vanīti bhagavā| atha vā ‘‘yai imai sīlādayau dhammakkhandhā sāvakaihi sādhāraṇā guṇakauṭṭhāsā guṇabhāgā, kinti nu khau tai vinaiyyasantānaisu patiṭṭhapaiyya’’nti mahākaruṇāya vani abhipatthayi, sā cassa abhipatthanā yathādhippaitaphalāvahā ahausi| aivampi bhāgai vanīti bhagavā|

‘‘yasmā ñaiyyasamāpatti-guṇabhāgai tathāgatau|

bhaji patthayi sattānaṃ, hitāya bhagavā tatau’’||

kathaṃ bhagai vanīti bhagavā? samāsatau tāva katapuñ‍ñaihi payaugasampan‍naihi yathāvibhavaṃ bhajīyantīti bhagā, laukiyalaukuttarasampattiyau| tattha laukiyai tāva tathāgatau sammāsambaudhitau pubbai baudhisattabhūtau paramuk‍kaṃsagatai, vani, bhaji, saivi, yattha patiṭṭhāya niravasaisatau buddhakaradhammai saman‍nānaintau buddhadhammai paripācaisi, buddhabhūtau pana tai niravaj‍jaisu upasaṃhitai anañ‍ñasādhāraṇai laukuttaraipi, vani, bhaji, saivi, vitthāratau pana padaisaraj‍ja-issariyacak‍kavattisampatti-daivaraj‍jasampattiādivasaina jhāna-vimaukkha-samādhisamāpatti-ñāṇadassana-maggabhāvanā-phalasacchi-kiriyādi-uttarimanussadhammavasaina  ca anaikavihitai anañ‍ñasādhāraṇai, bhagai, vani, bhaji, saivi| aivampi bhagai vanīti bhagavā|

‘‘yā  tā sampattiyau laukai, yā ca laukuttarā puthu|

sabbā tā bhaji sambuddhau, tasmāpi bhagavā matau’’||

kathaṃ sattavāti bhagavā? bhattā daḷhabhattikā assa bahū atthīti bhattavā| tathāgatau hi mahākaruṇāsabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇādi-aparimitanirupamappabhāva-guṇavisaisasamaṅgibhāvatau sabbasattuttamau, sabbānatthaparihārapubbaṅgamāya niravasaisahitasukhavidhānatapparāya niratisayāya payaugasampattiyā sadaivamanussāya pajāya ac‍cantupakāritāya dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇaasīti anubyañ‍janabyāmappabhādi anañ‍ñasādhāraṇavisaisapaṭimaṇḍitarūpakāyatāya yathābhuc‍caguṇādhigataina ‘‘itipi sau bhagavā’’tiādinayappavattaina laukattayabyāpinā suvipulaina suvisuddhaina ca thutighausaina saman‍nāgatattā uk‍kaṃsapāramippattāsu appicchatāsantuṭṭhiādīsu suppatiṭṭhitabhāvatau dasabalacatuvaisāraj‍jādi-niratisayaguṇavisaisa-samaṅgībhāvatau ca rūpappamāṇau rūpappasan‍nau, ghausappamāṇau ghausappasan‍nau, lūkhappamāṇau lūkhappasan‍nau, dhammappamāṇau dhammappasan‍nauti aivaṃ catuppamāṇikai laukasan‍nivāsai sabbathāpi pasādāvahabhāvaina samantapāsādikattā aparimāṇānaṃ sattānaṃ sadaivamanussānaṃ ādarabahumānagāravāyatanatāya paramapaimasambhattiṭṭhānaṃ| yai tassa auvādai patiṭṭhitā avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatā haunti, kainaci asaṃhāriyā taisaṃ pasādabhatti samaṇaina vā brāhmaṇaina vā daivaina vā māraina vā brahmunā vā| tathā hi tai attanau jīvitaparic‍cāgaipi tattha pasādaṃ na paric‍cajanti, tassa vā āṇaṃ daḷhabhattibhāvatau|

tainaivāha –

‘‘yau vai katañ‍ñū katavaidi dhīrau|

kalyāṇamittau daḷhabhatti ca hautī’’ti|| (jā॰ 2.17.78)|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddau ṭhitadhammau vailaṃ nātivattati, aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yaṃ mayā sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ, taṃ mama sāvakā jīvitahaitupi nātik‍kamantī’’ti (udā॰ 45; cūḷava॰ 385) ca| –

aivaṃ  bhattavāti bhagavā niruttinayaina aikassa ta-kārassa laupaṃ katvā itarassa ga-kāraṃ katvā|

‘‘guṇātisayayuttassa, yasmā laukahitaisinau|

sambhattā bahavau satthu, bhagavā taina vuc‍catī’’ti||

kathaṃ bhagai vamīti bhagavā? yasmā tathāgatau baudhisattabhūtaupi aparimāṇāsu jātīsu pāramiyau paripūraintau bhagasaṅkhātaṃ siriṃ issariyaṃ yasañ‍ca vami uggiri, khaiḷapiṇḍaṃ viya anapaikkhau chaḍḍayi, carimattabhāvaipi hatthagataṃ cak‍kavattisiriṃ daivalaukādhipac‍casadisaṃ catudīpissariyaṃ cak‍kavattisampattisan‍nissayaṃ sattaratanasamuj‍jalaṃ yasañ‍ca tiṇāyapi amañ‍ñamānau nirapaikkhau pahāya abhinikkhamitvā sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhau, tasmā imai sirīādikai bhagai vamīti bhagavā| atha vā bhāni nāma nakkhattāni, taihi samaṃ gacchanti pavattantīti bhagā, sinairuyugandharauttarakuruhimavantādibhājanalaukavisaisasan‍nissayā saubhā kappaṭṭhiyabhāvatau, taipi bhagavā vami tan‍nivāsisattāvāsasamatik‍kamatau tappaṭibaddhachandarāgappahānaina pajahīti| aivampi bhagai vamīti bhagavā|

‘‘cak‍kavattisiriṃ yasmā, yasaṃ issariyaṃ sukhaṃ|

pahāsi laukacittañ‍ca, sugatau bhagavā tatau’’||

kathaṃ bhāgai vamīti bhagavā? bhāgā nāma sabhāgadhammakauṭṭhāsā, tai khandhāyatanadhātādivasaina, tatthāpi rūpavaidanādivasaina pathaviyādivasaina atītādivasaina ca anaikavidhā, tai ca bhagavā sabbaṃ papañ‍caṃ sabbaṃ yaugaṃ sabbaṃ ganthaṃ sabbaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ samucchinditvā amatadhātuṃ samadhigacchantau vami uggiri, anapaikkhau chaḍḍayi na pac‍cāvami| tathā haisa ‘‘sabbatthakamaiva pathaviṃ āpaṃ taijaṃ vāyaṃ, cakkhuṃ sautaṃ ghānaṃ jivhaṃ kāyaṃ manaṃ, rūpai saddai gandhai rasai phauṭṭhabbai dhammai, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, cakkhusamphassaṃ…pai॰… manausamphassaṃ, cakkhusamphassajaṃ vaidanaṃ…pai॰… manausamphassajaṃ vaidanaṃ, cakkhusamphassajaṃ sañ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… manausamphassajaṃ sañ‍ñaṃ, cakkhusamphassajaṃ caitanaṃ…pai॰… manausamphassajaṃ caitanaṃ, rūpataṇhaṃ…pai॰… dhammataṇhaṃ, rūpavitak‍kaṃ…pai॰… dhammavitak‍kaṃ, rūpavicāraṃ…pai॰… dhammavicāra’’ntiādinā anupadadhammavibhāgavasainapi sabbaiva dhammakauṭṭhāsai anavasaisatau vami uggiri, anapaikkhaparic‍cāgaina chaḍḍayi| vuttañhaitaṃ ‘‘yaṃ  taṃ, ānanda, cattaṃ vantaṃ muttaṃ pahīnaṃ paṭinissaṭṭhaṃ, taṃ tathāgatau puna pac‍cāvamissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jatī’’ti (dī॰ ni॰ 2.183)| aivampi  bhāgai vamīti bhagavā| atha vā bhāgai vamīti sabbaipi kusalākusalai sāvaj‍jānavaj‍jai hīnapaṇītai kaṇhasuk‍kasappaṭibhāgai dhammai ariyamaggañāṇamukhaina vami uggiri, anapaikkhau paric‍caji pajahi, paraisañ‍ca tathattāya dhammaṃ daisaisi| vuttampi caitaṃ ‘‘dhammāpi vau, bhikkhavai, pahātabbā, pagaiva adhammā (ma॰ ni॰ 1.240)| kul‍lūpamaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, dhammaṃ daisaissāmi nittharaṇatthāya, nau gahaṇatthāyā’’tiādi (ma॰ ni॰ 1.240)| aivampi bhāgai vamīti bhagavā|

‘‘khandhāyatanadhātādi-dhammabhāgā-mahaisinā|

kaṇhasuk‍kā yatau vantā, tataupi bhagavā matau’’||

taina vuttaṃ –

‘‘bhāgavā bhatavā bhāgai, bhagai ca vani bhattavā|

bhagai vami tathā bhāgai, vamīti bhagavā jinau’’ti||

dhammasarīraṃ pac‍cakkhaṃ karautīti ‘‘yau vau, ānanda, mayā dhammau ca vinayau ca daisitau pañ‍ñattau, sau vau mamac‍cayaina satthā’’ti vacanatau dhammassa satthubhāvapariyāyau vij‍jatīti katvā vuttaṃ| vajirasaṅghātasamānakāyau paraihi abhaij‍jasarīrattā| na hi bhagavatau rūpakāyai kainaci sak‍kā antarāyaṃ kātunti| daisanāsampattiṃ niddisati vakkhamānassa sakalassa suttassa ‘‘aiva’’nti nidassanatau| sāvakasampattiṃ niddisati paṭisambhidāppattaina pañ‍casu ṭhānaisu bhagavatā aitadaggai ṭhapitaina mayā mahāsāvakaina sutaṃ, tañ‍ca khau mayāva sutaṃ, na anussutaṃ na paraṃparābhatanti imassatthassa dīpanatau| kālasampattiṃ niddisati bhagavāsaddasan‍nidhānai payuttassa samayasaddassa buddhuppādapaṭimaṇḍitabhāvadīpanatau| buddhuppādaparamā hi kālasampadā| tainaitaṃ vuc‍cati –

‘‘kappakasāyai kaliyugai, buddhuppādau ahau mahacchariyaṃ|

hutāvahamajjhai jātaṃ, samuditamakarandamaravinda’’nti||

bhagavāti daisakasampattiṃ niddisati guṇavisiṭṭhasattuttamabhāvadīpanatau garugāravādhivacanabhāvatau|

avisaisaināti  na visaisaina, vihārabhāvasāmañ‍ñaināti atthau| iriyāpatha…pai॰… vihāraisūti iriyāpathavihārau, dibbavihārau, brahmavihārau, ariyavihārauti aitaisu catūsu vihāraisu| samaṅgīparidīpananti samaṅgībhāvaparidīpanaṃ| aitanti ‘‘viharatī’’ti aitaṃ padaṃ| tathā hi taṃ ‘‘idhaikac‍cau gihīhi saṃsaṭṭhau viharati sahanandī sahasaukī’’tiādīsu (saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.241) iriyāpathavihārai āgataṃ| ‘‘yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharatī’’ti (dha॰ sa॰ 160; vibha॰ 624) aittha dibbavihārai| ‘‘sau maittāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharatī’’tiādīsu (dī॰ ni॰ 1.556; 3.308; ma॰ ni॰ 1.77; 2.309; 3.230) brahmavihārai| ‘‘sau khau ahaṃ, aggivaissana, tassāyaiva kathāya pariyausānai tasmiṃ aiva purimasmiṃ samādhinimittai ajjhattamaiva cittaṃ saṇṭhapaimi san‍nisādaimi aikaudiṃ karaumi samādahāmi, yaina sudaṃ nic‍cakappaṃ viharāmī’’tiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 1.387) ariyavihārai|

tattha iriyanaṃ vattanaṃ iriyā, kāyappayaugau| tassā pavattanupāyabhāvatau ṭhānādi iriyāpathau| ṭhānasamaṅgī vā hi kāyaina kiñ‍ci karaiyya gamanādīsu añ‍ñatarasamaṅgī vā| atha vā iriyati pavattati aitaina attabhāvau kāyakic‍caṃ vāti iriyā, tassā pavattiupāyabhāvatau iriyā ca sau pathau cāti iriyāpathau, ṭhānādi aiva| sau ca atthatau gatinivattiādiākāraina pavattau catusantatirūpapabandhau aiva| viharaṇaṃ, viharati aitaināti vā vihārau, iriyāpathau aiva vihārau iriyāpathavihārau| divi bhavau dibbau, tattha bahulappavattiyā brahmapārisaj‍jādidaivalaukai bhavauti atthau| tattha yau dibbānubhāvau, tadatthāya saṃvattatīti vā dibbau, abhiñ‍ñābhinīhāravasaina mahāgatikattā vā dibbau, dibbau ca sau vihārau cāti dibbavihārau, catassau rūpāvacarasamāpattiyau| āruppasamāpattiyaupi aitthaiva saṅgahaṃ gacchanti| brahmūnaṃ, brahmānau vā vihārā brahmavihārā, catassau appamañ‍ñāyau| ariyānaṃ, ariyā vā vihārā ariyavihārā, cattāri sāmañ‍ñaphalāni| sau hi aikaṃ iriyāpathabādhanantiādi yadipi bhagavā aikainapi iriyāpathaina cirataraṃ kālaṃ attabhāvaṃ pavattaituṃ sak‍kauti, tathāpi upādin‍nakasarīrassa nāma ayaṃ sabhāvauti dassaituṃ vuttaṃ| yasmā vā bhagavā yattha katthaci vasantau vinaiyyānaṃ dhammaṃ daisaintau, nānāsamāpattīhi ca kālaṃ vītināmaintau vasatīti sattānaṃ attanau ca vividhaṃ hitasukhaṃ harati upanaiti uppādaiti, tasmā vividhaṃ haratīti aivamaittha atthau vaiditabbau|
